2a1(sha): yon tan phrin las thugs//ye shwe rang byung bdag nyid de//ye shes rgya mtsho dpag med kyi//yon tan rdzogs pa'i ngo bor
2a2(sha): gnas/'og min chen po'i gzhal yas khang //rgyal ba kun gyi b+ha ga'i gnas//yon tan kun rdzogs dga' ba'i klong //tshang med ye shes chos dbyings
2a3(sha): kyi/ngo bos kun nas 'khyil ba la//tshad dpag med pa'i yon tan rgyas//rnam thar bzhi'i sgo khyung mdzes//'dod yon lnga'i phreng bas brgyan//rnam thar ro brgyad
2a4(sha): kyi rta bab can//nyi zla'i phrin pas mdangs phyung zhing //nyi zla'i pad+ma'i gdan stengs na//lnga phung dag pa'i sangs rgyas ni//rgyal po lnga zhes rnam par brtan//'byung ba
2b1(sha): lnga'i rgyal mo dang //zhal gsum phyag drug zhabs bzhi yis/rnam par 'phrul pa'i tshul gyi bzhugs//rnam shes dbang po yul dang dus//phyi nang sems pa'i yab yum rnam//
2b2(sha): /rang rang mdog dang cha lugs rnams//bcu gsum cha lugs sgeg pa yas//nyi zla 'bar ba'i gdan la bzhugs//lus kyi chos bzhi 'joms pa ni//sgo bzhi gnas pa de dag
2b3(sha): kyang //btsun mo 'jigs pa'i tshul dang bcas//'bar bzhi rngam pa'i tshul gyis bzhugs//ye shes rgya mtsho'ig min gyi//yon tan rdzogs pa'i ngo bor bsten//'khor lo
2b4(sha): 'bar ba'i lta ba la//bdag po kun bzang 'khor lo bskor//bka' sdod skyes bu mdun na gnas//brjod pa med bzhin go bar mdzad//dpag med ye shes yongs rdzogs pas//
2b5(sha): zhing 'khor longs spyod phun sum tshogs//dga' bde yang dag rdzogs pa'i gnas//phyogs bcu dus bzhi rdzogs pa yis//ma lus 'dus pa'i gzhal yas khang //bdag po
3a1(sha): #rgyun dang bcas pa nyid//'jig rten gnas kyi 'og min las//nyis 'gyur shin tu dpag ma pa'i gnas//de bzhin thugs kyi khyad par las//ngo bos mtha' gsum las
3a2(sha): 'das pa//ma spang rgyan rdzogs gzhal yas khang //yon tan lnga'i bdag nyid nges//gru bzhi spyi bos 'khyil snang ba//de bzhin thugs kyi khyad par las//rang snang rgyal ba sems sogs dang /dang po'i
3a3(sha): sangs rgyas sar rgyal ba//sems dpa' chen po 'joms pa che//brjod kyi mi yang tshad dang //rnam gnyis tshul du bzhugs pa'o//'khor gyi nong na bskul mchog ldan//'dul ba'i dus bab gsang ba'i
3a4(sha): bdag/lag na rdo rje bsdu ba mdzad//de tshe dus kyi mtha' bral phyir//ngo bo dus bzhi mnyam pa nyid//rgyal ba'i sems dpa' rnal 'byor pa//dgongs pa'i rig pa rna bar brgyud//gsang ba'i snying po de kho na//nyid
3a5(sha): de nges pa'i dbyings nas phyung //sgyu 'phrul dra ba mngon rdzogs pa'i//bsten pa rgya mtsho sems kyi lung //rang byung rang snang dgongs pa nyid//byin rlabs dgongs pa rang shar bas//sems nyid rang snang
3a6(sha): gzugs brnyan de//rtog ge byis pa'i yul min pas//rgyal sras sems dpa' rang nyid la//nyid la 'chad cing nyid kyi 'grel//gsang mdzod sgyu 'phrul dra ba 'di//bstan pa kun gyi rtsa bas te//rang 'byung
3b1(sha): ye shes cig po nyid/ma lus tshad khyab par gsal//gsang ba'i theg le chen po ni//yon tan spyan rdzogs bdag nyid de//d+harma dkra chos kyi gnas//'od bzhi e sogs dbyings su gnas/mi shig thig la bam dbyibs can//nga la bde ba gsal byed bdag
3b2(sha): ma na yid sogs rnam shes bzhi//ye shes myong ba sdus bshad pa'o//ye shes na mkha'i gzugs skyes pa//ba ra ma ta don zab mo//bde ba dus gnyis snga phyi bral//dus gsum kun gyi ngo bor gnas//rgyal ba'i bzhug swa b+ha ga nyid//las dang yon tan rdzogs
3b3(sha): pa'i gnas//thig le byang chub sems nyid de//rang byung yis kun gyi ngag/chags pas khyab pa'i yod can la//rtog bcas mnyam du gnas pa'i 'khor//lhan cig skyes pa'i bde chen po//brjod pa med pa'i tshul gyis myong //byed pa rlung gi mtha' sdus pas//ma lus lhan
3b4(sha): cig skyes par shar//dus la 'jug ldog med pa'i dus//bde chen stogs pa zad pa'i dus//rgyal ba'i mdzad pa bsam mi khyab//gang la gang ltar bde 'dul ba'i//spyod pa rgya mtsho de nyid de//dpag med ye shes ngo bor shar//dus cig yon tan lhun rdzogs pas//mnyam nyid res 'jog
3b5(sha): med par gnas/zhes nyid la nyid bshad do//sgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho'i rgyud las gleng gzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o///de nas de bzhin gshegs pa nyid//sangs rgyas 'dus pa'i ngo bo nyid//de nyid bshad pa'i dgongs pa yis//'jug pa'i dge ba mnyam sbyor
3b6(sha): bas ta/ro cig mnyam pa'i ngo bor gyur//phyogs bcu dus bzhi ma lus pa//cig dang du mar dbyer med pas//dgongs pa'i byin rlabs nyid la nyid//brjod cing gsungs pa'i don nyid ni//phung po khams dang skye mched dang //phyi nang snod bcud ma lus pa//thabs dang shes rab dbyer med
3b7(sha): pa'i da lhag pa kun rdzob nyid sdo bas/rgyal mtshog btsun mo rang bzhin gyis//rgyal ba'i tshigs la dbang sgyur byed//phyogs 'acu snang bcung ma lus pa/thabs dang shes rab byer med pa'i/lhag pokun rdzo ba nyid brjod pas//rgyal ba'i tshogs la dbang bsgyur bya'i/phyogs
4a1(sha): @#//bcu snod bcud ma lus pa//bde chen thig le rang bzhin te//thig le dbyibs dang mtshon las 'das//bde ba mchog gi snying por gnas//de nyid gsang ba'i dbang nyid de//rgyal ba sras bcas myong dgyes nas//thig le bde chen nyid la gnas//de nas
4a2(sha): thabs dang shes rab nyid//ma lus 'dus pa'i ngo bo yis//mthong ba'i don nyid bstan bya'i phyir//gnyis med thabs kyis nyams myong ba yis//chags dang chags bral rnam par spangs//lhan cig skyes pa'i ngo bo nyid//gang du skyes dang 'gag pa dang /gnas dmigs 'gro
4a3(sha): 'od las 'das pa'i//de bzhin nyid ni bstan par mdzad//nyi zla dngas pa'i rgya mtsho ltar//yongs dgyes khyab par gyur pa'o//ma 'dres gsang ba'i snying po nyid//kun kyang gsum du gnas pa ste//rang bzhin thabs kyis mngon byas phyir//ma lus gsum gyi rang bzhin nyid//
4a4(sha): 'jug pa thabs kyi rang bzhin ni//thun mongs tha ma la kun spangs pa'i//thabs dang cho ga las 'byung bas//dam pa'i rigs la 'jog par mdzod//rig dang rig gsungs pa yis//ngo bo nyid ni gnyisu 'gyur//sems lnga skyes pa'i tshul nyid ni//skyes pa'i gzhi dang skyes pa yis///
4a5(sha): rigs cig rigs bzhi gang phye ba//'jug pa'i ngo bo stobs las ni//rigs kyi dbye bas lam dbye bas lam dbye ba//lnga dang gcig gi rgyu 'bras 'gyur//ma nges spyi'i dbye ba ste//bzhin nges 'byung thun mong min//de nyid phyi nang cha snang bas//phyi rol stobs pa'i thun mong la
4a6(sha): grub pa phyi nang rgyu 'bras phyir//rgyu 'bras phyi nang 'brel bar 'dong//skabs 'dir bzhi gsum mthun mong gnyis//phan chad sbyangs pa TI'i phyir//rang bzhin sngon du song ba dang //cheng du sngon du bskur bar 'gro//des na thun mong sngon rdzogs yul//gzhi dang skabs nas skye 'gyu
4a7(sha): ba/grol thar lam du rnam par bshad//de'ang cig char rim 'jug pa//snod kyi rim pas rnam par snang //gang yang sems lngas sdus pas te//g.yo ldang smon 'jug mthar phyin pas//lam bzhi khyad par so sor phye//rang bzhin cig las mngon gyur pa'i//thabs rkyen mos
4b1(sha): bas tshul bzhin du//lam bzhi sems lngas mthar phyin pa//mos dang mthar phyin tshul gyis snang //dngos po rang bzhin rtogs pa ni//bsnyen bsgrub bzhi dang sgo bzhi dang /ngag mnyam rtogs pa bzhi ngag gis//dus gcig rdzogs shing smin par byed//rnal 'byor//
4b2(sha): lnga la rim goms pas//mthar phyin lam la the tsom med//stong pa nyid la gang goms nas//snying rje rgyu ma'i tshul du bsgoms//phyag rgya'i rna lhor mngon bsal nas//rnal 'byor spros la the tsom med//rang rang spong dang ldan 'gyur nas//bdag gzhan mthar pa ni 'gyur ba'i
4b3(sha): lam ka gang du chags pa'i khyad par gyis//rten 'brel lus kyis bsgom pa ni//bye brag rlung las gyur pa ni//yis 'byung ba steng 'og sgo//dgug dang dgugs pa'i thabs nyid de//'khor lo bzhi dang srog shing gsum//me rlung 'gro bas na mkha'i khams//gzho ba steng du rnar grag/
4b4(sha): lkug pa gsum gyi sems lnga'i rim//skyes pa bcu drug sa thob pa'i//rang bzhin lhan cig skyes pa'i phyir//'chi ba nyid kyis thar pa'i lam//yul dang dbang por sbyor nus pa'i//chags pa'i lam gyis gang rtogs pa//dug gis de'i sel ba ltar//thabs dang ldan pa'i khyad
4b5(sha): bar yin//gang zag thabs dang sbyor ba yis//gang la de ltar thabs nyid kyis//'ching ba thabs kyis dkrol ba ltar//lung dang man ngag rtog pas 'grub//dam pa nyid la mnyes zin nas//yis rig pa las las gyur//dbang po rim par 'gags pa las//bden pa
4b6(sha): gnyis med 'char bar 'gyur//gsang ba yis chen po ni//thig le rgyu ba kun zad pa//de nyid sgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho ste//dman dang bcings dang dbang bsgyur bral//rtog rnams kyi spyod yul min//gang du yis shar ba yis//ma lus dam pa yi su//phyogs med rgyal ba nyid du 'gyur//
4b7(sha): /de nas kun mkhyen rgyal ba nyid//sras dang btsun mor bcas pa nyid//ma lus cig tu mngon gzigs kyang //thabs bral byang chub snying por ma rig pas//'gro ba lnga'i bde sdug khyad par rnams/skad cig ma 'dres gsal mkhyen nas//thabs kyis so sor dgrol ba'i phyir//
6a1(sha): kyi mad kyis pags/ chen pag sapa sa cig/lung gas pargya pag TI ci gsung de na cad la bcugag ban na sa ma dugs kyi sa rgya sa/ @#
6a2(sha): bla'i tshul gyur pa//de nyid de bzhin gshegs pa nyid//'dul ba'i dbang gis phyogs bcu yis//'jig rten drug gi rdul nyid du//sprul pa thub pa chen po yis//gang bdag du ni rnam mdzad//sku dang gsung thugs bsam mi khyab//'dul ba bzhi'i khyad par gyis//rang
6a3(sha): khes dang bsam pa dang /dbang po mos pa'i khyad par gyis//gdul ba'i bsam pa sna tshogasu//sgyu 'phrul dra ba snang mdzad de//rgyu dang thabs kyis grol ba yis//'gro ba ma lus bde ba dang //rig dang ldan pas thar pa yis//'bras bu don la 'khod par mdzad//mtha' thug med
6a4(sha): pa'i 'jig rten du//rgyal ba dus gsum mnyam bzhugs pas//de las 'byung ba'i sprul pa'i tshogs//rgyal ba kun kyang de bzhin no//ces nyid la nyid ba shad//sgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho'i rgyud las//sems lnga bstan pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o///
6a5(sha): de bas kun mkhyen dgyes chen po//'khor gyis dkyil 'khor kun gzigs nas//lhan cig go cha'i 'khor rnams la//byin rlabs dam tshig dbang thob nas//dbang dngos rang bzhin bstan par mdzad//ma lus chos rang bzhin ni//byang chub snying po rang bzhin la//dbyer med don du kun
6a6(sha): gnas kyang //lhan cig skyes pa'i ma rig pa//la rten kun tu brtags pa yis//ma rig gzungs 'dzin ngo bor smin//rten 'brel gnyis kyi tshul gyur bas//myong dang spyod yul so sor 'gyur// mkha'i rang bzhin dbyer med ltar//ma lus de bzhin nyid 'di la//rtogs pa'i
6a7(sha): dang bye ba la/bang ga dang gan dang yong ma yin/yang du dnganapa rtog ge bas/mi shin rtog pa'i snamams kyis/'ang bas gnan du bas 'dod pa'i khobs pade bskal par byed par 'ang /sangangs rgyas ris dri med pa/dril ma nyid du mi 'byung bas/slob por
6b1(sha): /man ngag thabs kyis//sgribs pa'i rang bzhin snang bar 'gyur//kun mkhyen dbang dang man ngag ces//byin rlabs thabs kyis phan 'dogs pa//'gro la yid bzhin nor bu gyur//sna tshogs bsam pa rangs mdzad pa//dman pa'i sbyor ba la rten nas//sangs rgyas labyor/
6b2(sha): lo 'char byed pa//lus dang longs spyod sna tshogs pas//bskal pa rdul gyi grangs mnyam par//bskur kyang drin dang bral gyur na//lan 'ga' tso gyis ga la yin//dang dang dmigs ldan pa yis//rang rang sbyangs ltar mngon gyur nas//dman pa'i lta ba spong byed cing /burtul zhugs rig
6b3(sha): nges gang zag pa//ji nyid ni mkha'i mtha yun du//btson zhing brtsal bas mi snyed de//gdung ba gsum las gdung ba de//mthong ba'i thabs ni gsum du bshad//lung dang man ngag cho ga yis//rtogs nas skye ba 'di yis 'grub//rnam smin bcas pas 'grub ngas na/rang dbang lus/
6b4(sha): kyi smos ci dgos//rgyal bas ma lus kun gzigs shing //thugs rjes the tsom mi slu bas//nges par bstan pa 'grub pa ni//dgongs pa rtogs na nges pa 'grub//rang rang blo dang mos pa yis//ji ltar 'dul zhing thabs mkhas pas//tshe dang bskal pa'i grangs bral zhing //lo
6b5(sha): zla bar brtsams pas 'grub//shes rab rkyen dang thabs ldan pas//ma lus dbyer med rtogs goms na//rang la rang snang gom pa yis//ma byung rang las mngon du snang //gang du lha dang spyod bral ba//ma bcos rang rig byang chub sems//byis pa rtog ge'i spyod yul min//dam pa
6b6(sha): rnams kyis nges par mthong //chos rnams rang bzhin rkyen bral ba'i//mi rtogs 'od gsal dbyer med pas//kun gyi ngo bo de bzhin nyid//da lta nyid du snang ba'o//don dam bden pa la sten nas//rgyu ma'i phyag rgya rang snang ba//goms nas sdus pa'i thig le nyid//ma btsos rang bzhin mya ngan
6b7(sha): 'das dbyir yul sems 'dres pa'i drung /rgyab rigs pa'i thig le nyid //rang 'byung stong par gnas 'gyur te//byin nga ngos rgyu ba med//ma bcos de bzhin nyid du mthong /thabs dang shes rab rtogs pa yis//ny-i bla 'dres pa'i gang thabs kyis//ma skyes mnyam la gnas 'gyur
7a1(sha): zhing ta/pung sogs gnas pa'i chos rnams kun//bde chen snying po gsal 'gyur zhing //dgag bsgrub med pa'i thabs las byung //spyod yul ma lus thang kun//ma skyes dbyer med mngon du snang //rta sgra mthong ba med pa'i don//ma skyes sgra yAM/
7a2(sha): mi g.yo ba//ka sgras thugs rjes rnal 'byor spyod//a las dbyug ldan gar sbyor ba//dran pa gcig pa rang nyid smra//des sogs byed pa'i rnal 'byor kun//ma skyes dbyer med mngon du snang //de sogs byed pa'i rnal 'byor lnga//cig la ma lus rdzogs par g+sa/
7a3(sha): rig pa'i khyad par rtsal gyis snang //gang du ma bcos rang snang ba//ma lus sgyu ma 'jig rten du//thabs dang ldan pas dbang sgyur zhing //ma lus dbyer meng tshul du snang //yab yum shes rab bcu bcas rgyu//thabs kyi khams lnga ye shes gsal//bde skyid
7a4(sha): thig le ma rnams kun//sems mtha' rtogs pas gnasu thim//bcu gsum sangs rgyas bdag 'gyur ba//ngo bos gzhan nas thob pa med//rtsa bcud shes pa'i ngo bo nyid//thabs kyis yab yum ye shes gsal//de nyid rten can brtan lnga ltar//rang gnas
7a5(sha): thabs kyis bdag nyid du//nyon mongs sa bon kun kyang gnas//de phyir gzhan la rag ma lus//dbang po yul sems nyid med pa'i//shes rab thabs rol rgyu ma las//man ngag thabs kyis dbang sgyur nas//rang bzhin sgyu 'phrul chen po 'charakathabs dang thabs/
7a6(sha): byung 'brel ba nyid//tha'i kun gyi gzhir gnas pa//rang lus thabs kyis ngo bo la//rang rkyen ji bzhin mngon gyur pas//tshegs med rang log gyur pa yis//bden pa dbyer med mngon du mthong ka rang bzhin ye shes mtha' dril bas//thabs dang las can skyes bu
7a7(sha): yis ta spyod la ma gun tu 'a ba ba//'khor dang mya ngan 'das pa'i rgya//chags pa'i dang tshig blang ba ga/kun kyang zangs dang shes rab nyid//phan tshun dgyes pa'i thabs nyid ni//sems mtha' 'ong bas go phye nas//rang snang babs kyi gnas sbyar bas//zung
7b1(sha): 'jug thabs chen de las byung //gnyis med ye shes rje che//rgyu rkyen gzhan las byung ba min//rang 'byung thabs kyi ngo bo nyid//lus dang rtsa dang longs spyod pas//thang bde chen 'jig rten du//bsal byed thabs kyis snang ba las//rkyen gyis bzlog par mthong ba
7b2(sha): bzhin//gzhan las 'byung zhing snang ba med//phyogs dus dkyil 'khor ngo bo nyid//bde ba chen po ma skyes pa'i//seng ge'i lta bu byin gyis brlabs//gsung gis gnyis med gsal bar 'gyur//gsang gsum bzhis sgribs yis che//khams gsum 'dul
7b3(sha): ba'i thig le nyid//'dres pas lhun grub yis nyid//dkyil 'khor bsal snang brtag par dkar//ma lus thig le rang snang ba'i//ma bcos rang snang thabs rtogs nas//ma nor blta ba'i don rtogs na//rang 'byung 'bras bu rang las 'char//phyogs dus rgyal ba'i
7b4(sha): ngo bo nyid//mi zad 'khor lho/snang don//ma mthong rmongs pa'i 'khrul pa rnams//byin rlabs lha dang yon tan ltar//snang 'dod res 'jog sems 'dod sogs//gzhan las 'phags 'dod nor ba'i lam//'dongs pas na mkha' snyegs pa bzhin//nor pas ma nor snyed mi
7b5(sha): 'gyur tam bcos 'bras bu radu snang don//byin rlabs dgongs pa rtog goms pa yis//ma rtsal thabs kyi rang 'char ba//byin rlabs gdul bya'i snang bar zad//ces nyid la nyid bshad do//rgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho'i rgyud las//chos thad bstan la phabs pa'i le'u
7b6(sha): ste gsum pa'o//dngas bcom ldan dgyis chen po//sangs rgyas kun 'dus dag pa ste//cig dang du ma las 'das pas//'jig rten 'das dang 'jig rten pa'i//brjod bya gsung gi 'khor lo nyid//mngon phyung nas ni gsungs par mdzad//
7b7(sha): aka kha ga g+ha nga/tsa tsha dza dz+hanya/Ta dza Da d+ha na/ta tha da d+ha na/pa ba ba d+ha ma//ya wa ra la/sha Sha sa ha k+Sha/i AM/su u/e I/o o/a ka tsaDa ta pa ya sha a A//i I/u u/e ai/o au/ri rI l-i lI/a A/ka kha ga g+ha ba/
8a1(sha): mdzadz+ha&/TA rASh+Tha d+haN/tA ba d+ha na/ba pha ba b+ha ma/ya wA ra la/sha kha ga g+hA / d+ha!/TaTha nA nA Na/tamang d+han/ba pa ba rna ma ya wa ra la/shaSha sa ha/rang 'byung mtshan pa las 'das pa//a nyid kun gyi ngo bo te/brjod 'angs/
8a2(sha): namakha' rang bzhin las/snod bcud yi ger rang bzhin shar//yang yi ge rang bzhin ste//mtshon pa brda ngo bor snang //gang du ma skyes yum nyid ni//sa dang na mkha' can rdo rje//stobs chen lnga ni dbang po lnga//gnyis pa lnga po rims bzhin du//sgeg mo phreng thogs
8a3(sha): dbyangs dang gar//sa man+ta kritita nyi bshad //gsum pa lnga ni rim pa bzhin//snang mdzad rdo rje rin chen dang //dpag med don grub nyid du bshad//bzhi pa lnga pa rims bzhin du//sangs rgyas spyan dang na bza' dkar//mA ma ki dang sgrol mar bshad//lnga pa lnga po rim pa bzhin
8a4(sha): /byams dang sgrib sel kun bzang dpal//rta mgrin rim par 'dod pa yin//drug pa bzhi ste rim pa bzhin//bdug spos mar me me tog dang //dri chab lha mo zhesu bshad//bdun pa bzhi ste rim pa bzhin//rtug chad mtshan ma dag pa dang //bdag med ma ste rim pa bzhin//mthar
8a5(sha): zang byed pa rdo ni//ma lus 'dus pa'i ngo bo ste//sku gsung thugs ni rdo las//rnar 'phrul pa'i sprul pa drug/brgyad pa'i yi ge drug tu bshad//bcug gnyis pa rnam bshad pa//sgrib pa kun bzangs ye shes ni//'ching ba gso byed yin par bshad //thog ma mthar zad
8a6(sha): 'ching ka ste//rdo rje dril bu nyid du 'dod//dang po lnga dang gnyis pa lnga//gtso bo yab yum bcu nyid de//'khor lo rdo rje rin chen no//pad+ma ral gri 'dzin pa ste//dkar po mthing ka ser po dang //dmar po ljang khu rims pa bzhin//gsum pa lnga po rims pa bzhin//rdo rje dang ni rin
8a7(sha): chen dang //'dra rdo rje gar sngon dang //dmar dang ldang khur rang bzhin gnas/rje rnon po 'bring nag yon//bani ba lnga po rim pa bzhin//klu shing ba te rin chen ste//ut+pal dkar ser dpar ser dang /dmar ldeng dkur lngang rim bzhin te//thong srul ser nag 'bar bar gnas//
8b1(sha): tsa la sogs pa'i lha mo ni//me long phreng ba pi wang gar//gtso bo nyid dang mtshungs pa yin//gzhan yang ya sogs bzhir bshad pa//bdug pa me tog mar me dang //dri yang gtso bo rjes 'thun pa'o//zong sbrul 'jig pa dmar dag ste//de bzhin yi ge'ang shes par bya//
8b2(sha): rtag chang lcaT kya zhags pa dang //lcags sgrogs dril bu thabs dang 'dra//sprul pa'i pi wang sreg par dang /po ti sgrom dang go cha dang //me dang chur ni shes bya ste//dkar dang ser dang dmar nag dang //ser nag 'thing sngo dung kha dag/rim pa bzhin du shes par bya//rdo rje
8b3(sha): rgya khram ljang nag gis//ma lus gnon pa bdud rtsi 'khyil//dkyil 'khor 'khar lo yi ge yis//ma dad pa dag dad byed cing //ma mthong ba dag mthong byas nas//de bzhin rnar grol bar 'gyur//de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ni//rang snang gsung gi 'khor lo lnga
8b4(sha): rim bzhin rang gis bzhir snang ba//dpag med yon tan lhun rdzogs pas//rang 'byung 'khor lo mngon par brjod//rang 'byung 'khor lo rgyu ba'i ngagu/ka sogs sum cu rtsa gnyis ni//ma lus kun gyis dngos gzhi ste//sku'i 'khor lo rang snang ba'o//sgra
8b5(sha): dbyangs gsung gi 'khor lo ni//brjod pa'i sgra ni sna tshogs pa//ma lus par ni rang snang bas//gsung gis phyag rgya bzhir nges pa'o//rig pa thugs kyi 'khor lo ni//zag med yis drug ldan zhing //sde tshan brgyad kyis brgyan pa ni//snying po dngos ma lngar snang
8b6(sha): bo'o yon tan sprul pa'i 'byung bzhi nyid//bye brag brgyad cu kun 'dus pa'i//'phro 'du thig le kun gyis dngos//sprul pa'i thig le rang 'char ba//snang ba'i mdzad pa bsam mi khyab//gang du thebs las byung ba yis//dngas ma li ro lnga myong 'gyur/
8b7(sha): bzhis //rten bcas ngo bo dag gyur nas//mdzad pa'i byin rlabs bsam mi khyab//shes rab thabs rol rgyu ma las//rang bzhin rgyu 'phrul chen po 'char//thibs dang thabs 'byung 'brel pa nyid//thang kun gyi bzhir gnas pa//ma skyes sems kyi rang
9a1(sha): bzhin las//sna tshogs rgya ma'i don 'char bas//mangs la brten pa'i skyes bu nyid//gnyisu med par 'brel par bshad //gnas bzhi gar thabs sgyur ba las//na mkha'i les cir yang gsal//cir snang bdag nyid reg pa
9a2(sha): 'brel ta dra ba'i don du nga yis bshad//gsang ba'i 'khor lo rang 'byung ba//gnas dang ngo bor ldan pa yid//thabs dang rkyen las snang ba yis//ro lnga sems la mnyam 'jug pa//steng 'og thabs kyi mtshon byas pa//yis lnga'i ngo bor bshad//g.yas
9a3(sha): g.yon dbus dang ldan pa yis//nyi zla 'bar ba gang mdzad pa//thabs lhan gnas sems rtse yi bzung //yon tan kun gyi ngo bor gsal//ma lus 'dus pa'i byang chub sems//gnas lhan byed pa'i ngo bor bcas//skad cig thabs las mngon
9a4(sha): gyur pa'i//bde ba chen po'i ngo bor snang //rang 'byung snang ba'i mtshan ma kun//bde ba chen po'i don rtog pas//dngas ma'i snying por gsal gyur pa//bde chen na mkha'i mtha' ltar snang //gang du mtshan rtog kun spangs pa'i//bde chen yis ro
9a5(sha): dang bcas/thabs dang shes rab 'brel pa yis//ma lus sku gsum thugasu snang //thabs lhan yis rgya mtsho yis//chos kun ro cig kun gsal bas//rtog dang mi rtog cha las 'das//de nyid mtshon pa med par bstan//sangs rgyas 'khor lo kun
9a6(sha): 'dus pa'i//gsung gi dkyil 'khor mi zad pa//gdul bye'i don du kun bsgrags pa'i//rang 'byung 'khor lo'i gsung du gsal//phyi nang gsang ba'i mthar thug pa//snod bcud ro dang bcas pa las//mi rtog gsal bar rang snang ba//'khor lo kun gyi bzhi
9a7(sha): gyu ba la/sa nyid la nyid ba gang do//rgya phrul gyi mtsho'i rgyung gis//gsung gi 'khom mngon du blang ba'i le sa bani ///de nas bachim ldan kyis chen po//sangs rgyas kun cing bzhid rig//kun brgya sbya phru
9b1(sha): che ta dngos grub cho ga yang gsung ba//rang byung rgyu 'phrul dra ba ni//sangs rgyas kun gyi rang bzhin te//gsang ba'i ngo bo thabs kyi gnas//dngos grub kun gyi rtsa ba ste//sems nyid kun gyi rtsa ba yin//de nyid dngos med snying po ste//thad 'byung ba'i gnas
9b2(sha): mchog nyid//ma bcos rgyu 'phrul chen po las//gnyis med 'brel ba dra ba'i don//ro gcig gnas kyi khyad par las//yon tan rang bzhin sna tshogs 'char//sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor ma lus pa//bzhi bcu rtsa lngar rang bzhin gnas//de nyid grangs las
9b3(sha): 'das pa ste//ma bcos rang rig byang chub sems//thabs dang shes rab gnyis yang ba'i//cho ga sna tshogs 'dod rnams la//rang rang mos pa ji bzhin du//cho 'phrul sna tshogs bstan par zad//rang byung yo byad dag pa ni//bskyed cing sbyangs nas 'bar bar ltar
9b4(sha): nges pa brnyed nas longs spyad nas//mtsho ba bdud rtsi'i ngo bo ltar//gang gis rang rig byang chub sems//cho 'phrul bsal goms rnal 'byor pas//rang byung lnga la byin rlabs te//zos pas phyi nang nad sel 'gyur//phyag rgya dkyil 'khor bsal goms
9b5(sha): nas/rang 'byung rtsang mi med sbyar zhing //bdud rtsi lnga dang sha lnga la//yi ge lde dang gsum gyi sbyangs//'dod pa phun sum tshogs pa 'gyur//rang gi goms pa las rung nas//ji ltar bsam pa kun 'grub ste//dngos po gang yang rung ba dag/gang ltar rgyu ba der 'grub
9b6(sha): ste//rtog goms stobs las 'byung ba'o//bdag nyid bsal bar bstan byas nas//mkha' dbyings rdo rje bltams pa las//bdag nyid 'grod par nus pa na//rnor bsal ba'i don rnams bya//gang gis spyan dang thugs bsal bas//mi 'thun ma lus kun
9b7(sha): bsreg nas//dngos med stong pa'i ngang du 'tshig/lag 'thil zla ba'i chu rgyun bsam//na mkha' sa bzhir bcas pa nyid//chu yis thang gang byas nas//bsreg pa'i skal pa gzhil bar bya//rang gis sgron ma'i rnal 'byor pa'i//brtan zhing goms par gyur pa na
10a1(sha): @#gling bzhi ri rab 'khor yug na//ma lus brlag cing 'thor bar 'gyur//yam gis bud cing khams gis dkyus//rigs sbyangs pas dngos po dag khadag gzhan rgyal snang ba rnams//dngos med stong pa ston par nus//mi mthun phyogs kyi dgra bgegs
10a2(sha): dang //sangs rgyas btsun mo gang yang rung //lha min gnod sbyin bu mo dag/ting 'dzin sbyor bas goms pa yis//de ma thag par dkug nas ni//bdag nyid ji ltar dga' bar byed//de nas 'dod pa'i don rdzogs nas//yid la der gnas mi 'dod na//gang dga'i gnasu
10a3(sha): btang nas ni//phyis ni dam bcas bzhin du 'grub//gal te phyogs par mi 'gyur kyang //bdag gis bya ba ma rdzogs par//sngags dang phyag rgya ting 'dzin gyis//ji srid 'dod par bcing bar bya//rang gi don ni legs grub nas//lus dang sems ni sgrol ba yis//
10a4(sha): pha rol ci bder gnas par bya//bdag dang gzhan dag phan 'dod pas//rim pa bzhin du brtsam par bya//bdag nyid thar lam 'grod pa yis//stong pa chen por goms gyur nas//rgyal ba'i sku tshe mi 'chi nyid//mtshan ma'i rgyun gyis tshe bsdus nas//'dod pa yid 'ong mos
10a5(sha): par 'gyur//steng 'og rlung la dbang thob nas//bar ma'i rkyen gyis slub med//mtshan bzang thig le bcu drug sogs//dben pa'i gnas su zla dang bcas//zhun chen la sogs bcud kyis gso//gang rgyas bdud rtsi gsang ba'i lces//rngubs nas mi 'che nges par myo/
10a6(sha): lnga'i rtsa ba yan lag bcas//bdud rtsi rigs brgya lnga cig sbyangs//rtag phyin 'thungadra na the tsom med//ra sa yan la sogs la//bsgrubs cing rten na de bzhino//che me chog las byas zla rgyas sogs//rtags ldan zos pa'ang nges par 'grub//gzhan pa tha mal la sbyar to///
10a7(sha): bdag nyid kun la nges par 'gro/sngon song ba'i snga rol nyid//yan na mthar nya'i lam che'o//tshe'i rten ni gang rung la//bdag nyid stobs ldan rnal 'byor pas//phyogs bcu gzi byin kun sdus nas/sngags dang phyag rgya ting 'dzin gyis/snyoms la nges par srung
10b1(sha): /bar bya/ gal te 'chi ba gso 'dod pas//rgyal rigs rje'u dang bram ze'i ro//rma med gtsang ma blangs nas ni//sbyangs shing rgyan dang ldan byas te//bdag gis/yin bkug nas ni//mngon stims rtag dang ldan gyur nas//mgo dang lce gtsang go mtshon gyis//lha mi
10b2(sha): g.yul las 'joms par byed//lce gcad stabs pas gser yin te//langs nas dam bcas chu srung nas//gang 'dod las ni bcol bar bya//gal te rag po rtsom gyur na//de'i bdud rtsi dag la brten//ming rus bcas pa gsal bar bya//drag po'i gnasu
10b3(sha): gzhag nas ni//gnyen pos gnas bkug stim byas nas//dug dang lan tshwa tsha bas stim//gnas lngar phur btab de nyid gnan//'di ni kun kyang brlag par 'gyur//dgra dpung 'pham pa'i las byas nas//bdag nyid lha ru gsal byas la//ras sam gro gar
10b4(sha): ming rus bris//chags pa'i gos kyis dril byas na//g.yas pas legs par mnan nas kyang //nga rgyal rang gis bsngags bcas pas//phyogasu 'phyongs pas 'pham par byed//de yis rgyal bar 'gyur ba ste//spyi gtsug 'khor lo rtsibs bcu gzhag/khro bo
10b5(sha): sngags gnyis byin rlabs nas//ma yengs gar phyin rgyal bar 'gyur//gzhan yang thabs dang ldan pa yis//thabs dang shes rab lhan skyed de//rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor nyi zlar blangs//gnyis thims nyi zlar bcud lngar 'dres//'thungs pas bdud rtsi dag par byed//
10b6(sha): de gnyis med ldan 'gyur nas//rtse spras byed pa'i thabs bcas pas//dug gsum nad gsal bde chen gyis//rang dga'i lus kyis sta bsu gnas//rlung sems bde bas gang 'gyur tshe//thun mongs thun mongs ma yin pa'i//'dod pa lnga la longs spyod cing //
10b7(sha): zhing dang spyod yul dag par 'gyur//gang tshe thabs dang ldan pa yis//tha mal snang ba lus bcas pa//dka' zhing gdung ba spangs pa yis//bde chen dbyer med nyid du 'gyur//rang rig 'od gsal nam mkha' ni//rdo rje pad+ma la rten pas//'byung gzhi thabs kyis 'ab
11a1(sha): @ba yis//tha ma la zhen pa mi gnas shing //byang chub sems kyis gang bar byed//sgrol ma rlung gi thabs ldan pas//'bar nas rim par gnas bskyod pas//steng gi thig le zhu ba'i sems//dngas ma lngar tham bsal ba'i tshe//stong nyid nam
11a2(sha): mkha'i rang mtshan nyid//dbang po yul sems ldog par 'gyur//rang 'byung bcud 'dul thig le nyid//thabs kyi phyag rgyas bkug byas nas//thabs dang shes rab thabs kyis bcing //rang 'byung bde ba'i nyams shar nas//rang rang gnasu btang ba'i tshe//
11a3(sha): rlung sems zhen 'dzin grol gyur nas//sems mtha' 'gro 'ong med pa'i tshe/yes rgyal mtshor bsal 'gyur nas//ma lus bde chen 'bar bar gnas//bdud bzhi ma lus pham pa yis//phyogs med rgyal ba'i dkyi lor du//ting 'dzin gsal bas byed par
11a4(sha): 'gyur te bde ba'i thig le rang snang bas//ming tshig tha snyad brjod bral bas//thabs dang shes rab thabs mtshon pas//bde chen rig pa'i thig ler 'gyur//thun mongs khyad par yo byad kyis//sngon du bya ba kun byas nas//rang gi thabs ldan rnal 'byor pas
11a5(sha): ji ltar bsgrub pa bzhin du 'grub//sangs rgyas kun gyis sku gsung thugs//rang 'byung bde chen rgya mtsho ni//byin brlabs kun gyis bdag nyid pas//kun la 'tshams pa bzhin du 'char//thig les bde ba myong 'gyur nas//bde ba'i thig le'i ngo bor
11a6(sha): gsal//gnyis med rang byung snying po la//bsal bar nyams kyis goms par bya//bde chen thig le'i ngo bo ni//rdo 'dzin pa'i bdag nyid de//rigs kyi bdag po kun las grol//mtha' dbus rgyu 'bras las 'das pas//thig le rang rig byang chub sems//
11a7(sha): 'gro 'ong dmigs 'dzin zhen las 'das//de bzhin nyid du gshegs pa yang //sna tshogs zhen las grol bar zad//thabs dang shes rab thig le yi//nyi zla gsang ba'i sems ldan pas//dngos rnams lhan cig skyes nus nas//sgyu 'phrul dra bar gsal bar
11b1(sha): 'char/ces nyid la nyid gsungs pa'o//rgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho'i rgyud las//phye ma bsgrub pa'i le'u ste lnga pa'o///de nas bcom ldan 'das dgyes chen pos//mtha' dbus med pa'i lhun grub pa//ye shes kun gyi bdag nyid nges//sku'i
11b2(sha): dkyil 'khor snang bar mdzad//gcig dang dum bral ba yi//rang 'byung ye shes phyogs med pa//yis lnga rdzogs snying po nyid//mu khyud snying pos yong su bskor//sangs rgyas kun gyis gzhal yas khang //ngo bo ji bzhin gsal gyur pa//che ba'i yon tan lha maH
11b3(sha): btadakyil 'khor kun gyi rang bzhin nyid//ye shes 'khor lo rA ba gcig/lhun grub thig les yongasu bskor//che ba yon tan phyogs med cing //thar pa'i grong khyer khyams ldan pas//rna raM mdzes pa'i sgo dang ldan//'dod yon lnga dang bzhugs gnas kyang //zad pa
11b4(sha): med pa'i tshul du gnas//bdud dang 'khor ba'i nyes pa yis//mi 'jigs stobs dang rdzu 'phrul dang //dbang dang 'phrin las lhun rdzogs pas//thabs dang shes rab skyon bral bas//rgyal ba kun gyi gnas pa'i gnas//rang 'byung rig pa'i 'phrul pa yis///
11b5(sha): mdzad pa rdzogs dang ma rdzogs dang /'gro ba'i don las ma 'dres pas//lha dang gdan sa khyad par dang //bzhugs tshul rgyan cha sna tshogs bstan//rang 'byung rgyal ba thams cad kyang //rang gi rtog pa der 'byung gi/rgyu 'bras dka' thub rtsol ba yis//gzhan
11b6(sha): byung tha dad chos dang bral//rang rig lhun gyis grub pa la//yon tan khyad par kun rdzogs pas//thabs chen gsang bas goms rtogs nas//che ba'i 'bras bur bdag nyid gnas//rgyu 'phrul dra ba'i dkyil 'khor ni//kun nas rgya yongs ma chad pa//phyogs
11b7(sha): bcu dus pa ni sku gsung thugs//ma lus bdag nyid byang chub sems//gdod nas ngag mnyam rdzogs pa yis//mi zad 'khor lor rang snang ba//ma lus thaMd dag pa yis//byar med bya las 'das pa yis//rang kyang rgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho che//skyes bu rab la
12a1(sha): de bzhin snang ta ma lus 'dul stobs khyad par gyis//thabs dang shes rab 'brel pa las//rab 'bring tha ma'i stobs bzhin du//dka' thub gdung ba spangs pas 'grub//thabs chen thabs las byung ba yis//gtso 'khor dbyer med
12a2(sha): dkyil 'khor ni//'khor ba dag byed maN+Dal//bsgoms pas rgyu 'bras mngon du 'byung //thabs chen rgya mtshos zin pa la//'khor ba'i rgya mtsho bos stong //'das dang ma 'das dbyer med pa'i//rang rig byang chub de bzhin nyid//rang rig byang chub skyon bral la/
12a3(sha): mos pa'i yon tan khyad par gyis//rang rang kha dog ji bzhin du//yon tan stobs las mngon snang ba//ma 'dres gsal bar shar ba nyid//rgyu 'phrul thabs kyi ngo bo ni//dra ba shes rab ma skyes dbyings//gnyis med gnas pa rtog gom nas//'bras bu rdzogs
12a4(sha): pa'i tshul du gnas//skal bar sbyangs dang rang gnas pa'i//blo'i bye brag ji snyed pa//thabs kyi rims pa de bzhin te//'bras bu lhun grub nyid du 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun gyis gnas gyur pas//gsang ba'i bzhugs gnas thig le'i rten//ngo bo mtha' bral
12a5(sha): gnyis med pa//che ba'i yon tan lhun rdzogs pa//sgo dang ldan te snying por rdzogs//thig le chen po'i rang bzhin la//'bras bu mtshan ma rang bzhin rdzogs//thabs chen nyi zla'i ro 'dres pa//yis dkyil 'khor gsal bar snang //sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor ma lus
12a6(sha): ba na sku gsung thugs gi phyag rgya nyid//ma lus nyi zla'i tshul gnas te//skal ldan thugs la 'jug par 'gyur//bde chen rig pa'i dkyil 'khor ni//ma lus 'dus pa byang chub sems//thig le rlung dang bral ba yis//rdo rje 'dzin pa nyid du 'gyur//gang
12a7(sha): dang gang du zhen bzung ba//bde chen thig ler zad gyur na/zhing dang spyod yul bcas pa dag/ma lus rig pa bde chen po//thabs dang shes rab dbyer med pa'i//gsang ba'i thig le kun gyis dngos//thad rang bzhin rdzogs pa'i phyir//thams cad kun gyis
12b1(sha): ngo bor gnas//stong 'og g.yas g.yon las byung ba'i//steng 'og thig ler 'brel pa'i bcud//nyi zla thabs dang shes rab nyid//gnyis med thab su 'brel pa yin//brjod bya'i yon tan kun 'dus pa//sangs rgyas kun gyis rang bzhin nyid//ma lus/
12b2(sha): kun dang 'brel pa'i phyir//sems lnga'i tshul gyis mngon du snang //thig le dang po nas rnams nas//ma skyes pade chen bcu drug mtha'//'gro 'ong med cing mthar phyin pa//rang 'byung dkyil 'khor lhun gyis grub//thog med bsags las gang gyur pa
12b3(sha): las dang 'du byed sgrib pa rnams//bde chen sku gsung thugs gyur nas//ma sgribs ye shes ngo bor 'char//bye brag brgyad nu sprul pa yis//thig le'i rkyen gyis 'dus gyur pas//ma lus mun pas snang ba bzhin//dngos byed thabs kyis snang byed pa//
12b4(sha): rang byung rang snang ngo bo nyid//bde ba chen po'i thig le nyid//phan tshun 'brel cing 'dres pa las//nyi zla 'khor bcas de dag kun//gsang ba'i thig le 'phro 'du med//bde chen thig ler lhun rdzogs pa'i//byang chub sems la dngas snying med//lus ngag
12b5(sha): sems kyis bya ba rnams//gang gis 'dul bar mi nus pa//byin rlabs rten 'brel las byung ba'i//bde ba chen po'i ngo bor 'gag/snang gsum las 'das thig le ni//phyogs 'dzin dmigs khams las 'das pa//rang rig dmigs zhen dag pa yI/
12b6(sha): 'dus byas zag pa'i bde ba med//sangs rgyas kun gyi thig le ni//'gro 'ong thig le rgyu ba dag/mkha' ltar dag pa'i byang chub sems//rkyen bral yis zag med de//'phel 'grib med pa'i tshul du gnas//rgyud chen rgyu 'phrul dra ba las//rang 'byung /
12b7(sha): rgya mtsho nyid//gdul bya mos pa'i bsam pa bzhin//thugs rje'i byin brlabs bsam mi khyab//ji snyed mthara' yas chos rnams ni//rgyal ba kun dang dbyer med pa'i//sku gsung thugs kyi ngo bo ste//dbyer med bde chen phyag rgyar gsal//gang du snang dang mi
13a1(sha): @g.yo ba'i//bya ba byed dang mi byed pa'i//rgyu 'phrul dra bar sngon rdzogs pa'i//thig le mchog snying por gnas//rten dang rten par bcas pa'i//rgyu 'phrul dra ba'i dkyil 'khor na//sna tshogs bsam pa las byung ba'i//dkyil 'khor chen
13a2(sha): bo 'bar bar 'gyur//zag med yis lhun rdzogs pa'i//gdul bya'i bya ba kun kyang spyod//ces nyid la nyid bshad do//sgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho'i rgyud las ni//dkyil 'khor yong su rdzogs pa'i le'u ste drug pa'o//rje 'dzin pa chen po ni//sangs
13a3(sha): rgyas kun gyis dngos gzhi ste//gsung gis 'dul ba'i don du ni//de dag 'khor lo bskor bar mdzad//rtsa ba cir yang 'gyur ba'i brum//rin chen ye shes sna tshogs pa//phyi nang mtha' dbus rgya bral ba'i//chos dbyings 'og min gzhal yas khang //rang
13a4(sha): rang sa bon bskyed pa dang //gnas dang byin gyis brlabs pa'i phyir//rdo rje 'dzin dang rgyal ba 'dzin//sangs rgyas nor bu pad+ma dang //shes rab 'dzin pa'i rim pa bzhin//'khor lo'i tshul bzhin gnas pas mdzad//rang rab sa bon ji bzhin pa'i//gnas dang skyed
13a5(sha): ba'i byin brlabs pa//nang dang sbyor ba las byung ba//lnga steng 'byung ba'i rang bzhin nyid//rang gi btsun mo sbyang bya ste//bde skyed 'khril ba'i tshul du gnas//ming gi dang po las 'byung ba//rnam shes bzhi'i mnyam nyid ni//gnas skyed thabs kyis byin rlabs
13a6(sha): bas//rang gi gan su de bzhin gshegs//thun mongs thun mongs ma yin pa//yul bzhi bde skyed btsun mo ste//sa bon gnas skyed thabs las byung //thabs dang 'brel ba'i tshul du bzhugs//skyed mched gnas kyi rang bzhin las//ming gi dang po
13a7(sha): las byung ba//gnas dang skye mchid la sogs pa'i//gnas ni ji bzhi gnas par 'dod //drun mong thun mong se ma yin pa'i//dus ba no'i btsun mo ring bzhin ni//dgyes pa bskyed pa'i snya mo ste//gnyis ming 'khril ba'i tshul du gnas/dkrugs pa
13b1(sha): bsdams dang bskyod pa dang //dgyes pa chen po'i rang bzhin nyid//tshim dang me tog gtor ba yis//gnyis med lhan cig gsal ba yis//ma lus khyab cing gsal bar byed//'di dag dbye dang dbab pa dang //bim dang bde bas tshim pa dang //rim
13b2(sha): pa bzhin du yang dag gnas//gang du rang rig sa bon las//gong ma ji bzhin gsal gyur pas//lus kyi chos bzhi'i ngo bo ni//ta kring sgo bsrung tshul du gnas//gzhan dag rang gi btsun mo ni//lha mo bzhi'i dang po las//thun mong thun mong ma yin
13b3(sha): papa'i ta mtshan ma 'joms shing 'khril bas 'khod//ma lus 'dus pa'i ye shes ni//dmigs pa gsum pa las 'das pa ste//yul gsum 'das pa'i 'dus ma byas//de nyid 'dus pa'i ye shes dbyings//kun gyis rang bzhin nyid ces bshad//thabra
13b4(sha): dang shes rab la sogs pa//thun mong thun mong ma yin pa'i//chags sogs mtshan ma drug po ni//sa bon gnas skyes las byung ba'i//sprul pa'i skur ni mngon par rdzogs//rang 'byung bsten gyis thig le nyid//phyag rgya bzhi'i sbyor ba las//dga'
13b5(sha): dang chags dang bde ba dang //ye shes nyid ni gsal bar 'byung //sems lnga ye shes lngar smin pa'i//ye shes thig le gnas 'phos nas//rang 'dod gnas las phog par mdzad//nyi zla rig pa'i thig ler bcas//phan tshun 'dres par gyur pa na
13b6(sha): lnga dang lngar ni mi phyed pas//rig pa'i me tog dor zhes brjod//gang gis gang la gsol btab pa'i//thug rjes byin brlabs sbyang byas nas//thugs bzhugs 'dres nas dbyer med pa//sras gyur dgongs pas gcig la gnas//rang 'byung
13b7(sha): dngos dang mi 'gal bzhin//'bras bu nyid las gzhan min pas//ma lus dkyil 'khor thig le rnams/bdag gis sri su bsgoms par bya//lam dang 'bras bu'i khyad par dang //rtog pa las 'od las byung //byi brlabs 'bras bu
14a1(sha): ngos 'brel bas// na gshegs par sras tshogs bsgoms/rang shim bcos pam nying pas//rtog pa'i ye shes byin bzlas cad/ma lus snang ngas rgya mam ste//dasan dang gas chang skyong yul tshan//ma dzum sra/
14a2(sha): gsung thugs sbyor ba'i//de nyid thabs dang shes rab ste//dbyer med thim pas phyag rgya bzhi//gnyis med 'dres pa'i tshul du gnas//gnas sngar thim pa'i thig le ni//sangs rgyas kun gyi rang bzhin te//maMs lnga'i dbang bskur byir//dbu rgyun
14a3(sha): tsong ban ldan par brjod//gsum yi ge'i rang bzhin nyid//sangs rgyas kun gyis byin garnangs pas//thim pas garda byin skyeng ba ste//sku gsung thugs kyi 'ang bzhino//dngas ma 'dus pa'i thig la la//ye shes spyan grub rdzogs lnga'i ngos/
14a4(sha): ma lus kun la rgyas btabs pas//yis lnga'i tshul du gnas//nyi zla 'dus pa'i thig le nyid//brdams dang thabs kyis rang bzhin las//gsang ba'i yes lnga'i ros//khyab pas gnyisu med par gsal//'khor lo gsum gyi thig
14a5(sha): le nyid//yi ge gsum du rang gnas pas//thabs kyi sbyor bas gnyisu med//sku gsung thugs kyi sbyor ba'o//g.yas g.yon thig le dbus gnas pas//yab yum bde ba'i thabs dang bcas//rang lus bde chen gsal 'bar nas/sku
14a6(sha): gsung thugs kyi phyag rgyar sbyang /gzhan yang gsung gi phyag rgya mchog/ji ltar 'dul ba de nyid du//rtog 'dul gnyen po thabs snang //de nyid mtshan mar bsam mi khyab//sangs rgyas ma lus phyogs bcu rnams//thugs rje yis sna tshogs
14a7(sha): kyis//gsung gis de ltar brtag pa'i phyir//gsung gi phyag rgya'i ngo bor shar//brjod dang brjid bya dbyer meng dang //rang 'thung gsung gi dkyil 'khor nyid//ma lus 'dus pa'i gsung mchog nyid//ji ltar 'tshams pa'i tshul du snang /sangs
14b1(sha): /rgyas kun gyis gsung nyid ni//ma lus kun dang dbyer med pas//rtog dang bsgoms dang brjod pa yis//byin rlabs bskyob pas sngagasu brjod//rgyud chen rgyu 'phrul dra ba las//thog mtha' med pa'i byang chub sems//gsung mchog phyag rgya mthu
14b2(sha): yas pas//ma lus 'dus pa'i ngo bo nyid//ji snyed phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten du//chos kyi phung po dpag med pa'o//'dul ba'i thabasu gsungs pa yang //rgyu 'phrul dra ba'i gsung du 'dus/zab mo rten cing 'brel 'byung ba'i//rin chen rgya
14b3(sha): mtsho rgyud chen 'di//sku gsung thugs dang bya ba dang //spyod dang rnal 'byor rgyud chen rnams//ma lus 'dus pa'i snying por gnas//sangs rgyas kun gyis byin brlabs pa'i//rang 'byung gsung nyid rang snang bas//bstan pa rgya mtsho'i rang bzhin
14b4(sha): teta thugs rje gdul bya'i don du snang //ces nyid la nyid brjod do//rgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho'i rgyud las//gsung gi dkyil 'khor mngon du 'byung ba'i le'u ste bdun pa'o///de nas bcom ldan 'das dgyes chen pos//lus rtog
14b5(sha): phung por bcas pa rnams//bde chen lhun grub byin rlabs phyir//thugs rje dkyil 'khor bshad par mdzad//thabs dang shes rab rang bzhin ni//rgyud 'phrul dra ba mtshan nyid de//bde chen thig ler mngon rdzogs pas//phyi nang snod btsung
14b6(sha): thung ni ta bde chen byang chub sems kyi rgyu//bde chen thig les rgyas btabs pas//'angar dka' rang bzhin mi shig pa//shes rab stong nyid nyi ma ste//rin chen gsang dkyil zla thig le//sems lnga gnyis sbyor byang chub sems//

15a1(sha): @#dngas ma thig ler gsal thim pas//bde ba'i thig le 'phro 'du med//rang byung bde chen 'od gsal ba//sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor lhun gyi grub//phung sogs rtogs pa ma lus kun//bde ba'i yis lnga ru/

15a7(sha): tshigs thabs dang 'bral ba'i rang bzhin la//bde chen phra rabs yongs rdzogs pas//yul dbang rnam shes dus dang bcas//phyag rgya bzhi sogs rnam pa kun//bde ba chen po'i ngo bor rtogs//phyag rgya thugs sogs gdod nas rdzogs//

15b2(sha): khata/bde chen phra rags yongs rdzogs pa//yul dbang rnam shes dus dang //phyag rgya bzhi sogs rnam pa kun//bde ba chen po'i ngo bor gnas//khams bzhi 'dus pa'i sku gsung thugs//rang 'byung thugasu rang dag pa'i//gnas drug lam gsu
15b3(sha): rang gnas pa//thabs kyi drag po mi 'byung ba'i//bzhu ba'i bde ba lte ba yis//ar thim sprul pa'i gsang thig la//yis nam mkha' 'byung ba'i rgyu//bde chen rgyu las char rgyun sems//rim par gnas thims khams gsum sa//yis

16a1(sha): @bsdams dang gzhang dbanal spyod dang //rgod ba sgrags dang 'dzin pa rnams//kha skyong dang phyogs bcur dgrong ba yis//ma chags pa na nyid te//chags pa smin cing rgyas par mdzad//rmongs dang rgyas brgyal bas kun rdzus te/ma

19a6(sha): /rgyun chang ba dang mi 'phel dang //rang rgyud pa dang byin med pa//'jig rten log pa mtha' gyur pas//'di dag rnar spang bar bya//de nas gang du mtshan pa yis//yi 'od phyag rgya la rten cing //rgyu ba rlung gi drong ldan pa//de ma thag par grol bar 'gyur//

19b1(sha): ma'i yul las 'das//bde ba chen po mi g.yo ba/bde ba chen po'i rang bzhin ni//ma skyes bden gnyis dbyer med pa'i//ngo bor ma lus kun gsal ba//mi 'gyur brtan par brjod pa yin//ska la dman thabs kyi bde spyod na//steng 'og thabs kyis gnas rtsal//shes rab//
19b2(sha): 'phrul gyis 'khor lo bskor//rgyas dang 'byor pa'i rtags byung na//rgyu ba bcang la 'khor lo spang //bde chen bar chad med 'dod na//thabs kyi rim bzhin bsgom pa dang //shes rab yum gyi de bzhin te//yang na gnyi su med par bya//rang rang thabs dang ldan pas brtsams/
19b3(sha): bar chad dag ni byung ba na//thabs dang ldan pas skyed par bya//lus la brten pas sems gang ste//phyi nang bde chen rgyas par bya//de nas na mkha'i mthar thug par//khyab dang bar chad med par gnas//goms pa'i rim dang byin brlabs las//mkha' gro
19b4(sha): gsum gyi snang thob nas//zhing khams phyogs bcu rdul rnyed bcas//bdo chen zag pa med par gnas//dang po byin brlabs gnang thob nas//rdo rje 'dzin pa mthar thug par//tshes nas bcu drug mthar byin ltar//man ngag stobs kyi shes par bya//lung dang rig/
19b5(sha): ba la sogs pa'i//yid spyod tshad ma ring spangs na//nyams myong tshad ma la rten pas//rim spangs tshe 'di nyid kyi 'grub//las dang bskal pa dman pa dang //bla ma thabs la yid kyis phyogs//log pa'i skye bo'i dbang song ba'i//gang zag 'di dag rnam par spangs//
19b6(sha): thabs dang ldan pa'i rnal 'byor gyis//mi spyad pa ni gang yang med//mi bya ba yang yod min te//bza' dang mi bza' cung zad med//'di la bsgoms dang mi bsgoms med//tha~Md mnyam pa nyid sbyor bas//bya dang mi bya kun las 'das//snang srid 'dod yon lngar
19b7(sha): gnas te//bdade chen rog mar kun gnas pa//ma lus ying 'drog tshim byed pa//sgo gsum phyogasu btang du meng //bya ming bya las 'das pa yis//rje'u mi dmigs de bzhin nyid/ma bcos rang rig yul dang bral//gang du'i sgying gang 'dzin med/
20a1(sha): bcos thabs kyis bder bzhag pas//rang 'byung yis mtha' dbus med//cig dang du ma kun bral bas//thams cad ma lus chos kyi sku//bden gnyis tha snyad las 'das pa'i//ma skyes rang rig byang chub sems//yi ge tshig
20a2(sha): dang ming spangs pa'i//brjod bya ma lus rdzogs par snang //ces nyid la nyid bshad do//rgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho'i rgyud las//snod byin gyis brlabs pa'i le'u ste bcu pa'o///de nas de bzhin gshegs pa nyid//cig dang du ma kun
20a3(sha): bral ba'i//dgyes pa chen po'i bdag nyid kyis//tshogs kyi dkyil 'khor bstan par mdzad//phyogs bcu dus bzhi sdus pa yis//sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor ma lus pa//tshogs gnyis las 'byung bsam mi khyab//rang 'byung 'khor lo mngon rdzogs
20a4(sha): pa//dngos po 'jig rten mtha' thugs pa//sku gsung rdo rje gyur pa ni//dngos por snang ba thad nyid//rang 'byung rgyu 'phrul rgya mtshor bstan//thabs dang shes rab dbyer med pa'i//phyi nang gsang ba'i 'jug pa yis//rtog pa bzhi la rtog goms na//ma lus
20a5(sha): lha rdzogs dbyer med snang //don dam kun rdzob kun dbyer med pa'i//tshogs nyid dbyingasu tshul cig pas//ma lus rang snang chos thang //ma lus byang chub sems rtogs//sku gsung thugs nyid rang snang ba'i//ma lus gsung gis rgyan du rtogs//thad gsung
20a6(sha): gi yi ge ste//thabs dang ldan pas rgyas btab so//de nyid de'i byin brlabs ste//gnyis med tshul gyis rang snang ba'i//byin brlabs ngo mtshar bsam mi khyab//rang 'byung rang snang chos 'di dag/gnyis med rang rig ma skyes par//gsal bar mngon
20a7(sha): gsum tho tsam med//rtogs pa kun gyis mchog tu bshad//rangs ma las 'byung thig le yis//dzin dang mthon khyad par las//'angs da ma 'das rang snang bas//bde chen nang du ro cog pa'o//rang 'byung 'khor lo rim pa las//gsung gis
20b1(sha): /dkyil 'khor rang snang ba//skyes dang ma skyes dbyer med pas//yig 'bru tshul du nges par brjod//gang du thabs kyis byin brlabs pas//gnas dang rtog pa'i ngo bo yis//rim pa'i tshul gyis byin brlabs pa'i//'di dag byin gyis brlabs nyid de//
20b2(sha): gsang ba de nyid nges pa yis//rim pa'i phyag rgya mngon gsal bas//sgro dang bkur ba zhi ba yis//de kho na nyid mngon du snang //thabs dang shes rab dbyer med pa'i//bde ba chen po'i ro nyid ni//ma lus 'dus pa'i rang bzhin te//gnyis med gnyis lnga
20b3(sha): gang gyur pa//de nyid thig le bdag nyid do//sgra dbyangs gsungs gi bstan pa rnams//gnas gzhi thig le'i byang chub sems//thabs dang shes rab gnyis med pas//mi zad 'khor lo gsum du snang //gnas dang thabs dang sbyar ba yang //gsang bI/
20b4(sha): byin brlabs rang snang ba//thams cad bde ba chen po ste//de'i byin brlabs nyid snang ngo //sgo dkur med pa'i bstan pa nyid//bde chen rlung gi sbyor thabs kyis//snang srid phyi nang chos rnam kun//bde chen rgyu mar mngon gsum snang //thabs dang shes rab
20b5(sha): gnyis meng pa'i//yul dang dbang po rnams shes dang //dus dang mtshan mar bcas pa yis//'dus babs nyid kyi rang bzhin gyis//thang rang gi thabs bzhin du//bde ba chen po'i dkyil 'khor snang //de nyid bsgrub pa'i yo byad lnga//ma nyams
20b6(sha): gsal bar rab spyad nas//bdag gzhan ji snyed snang ba rnams//ma lus dag pa'i ngo bor 'gyur//de tshe las kyi bzung ma bzhi//rig pa'i rtsal las grub pa ste//gnas skabs rim par sbyor ba yis//lhun grub nyid la the tsom med//bsnyen dang nye/
20b7(sha): bsnyen sgrub pa dang /bsgrub pa chen po'i rim pa yis//sbyor ba bzhi ni brtsams par bya//bde chen thugs kyi dkyil 'khor gyis//rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor ma lus pa//dgyes snyam thim po'i ro lhan pa//thabs dang ldan pas blang sbyang bas//gang
21a1(sha): @#'dod 'bras bus phun gsum tshogs//bdag zhan phyi nang las byung ba'i//ma lus bsgral bya thams cad ni//so so bya ba kun snang yang //kun gyi rang bzhin de bzhin nyid//bdag nyid gsal bar mngon bsgoms te//ting 'dzin thab/
21a2(sha): kyis mngon bsgral te//'jig pa med pa'i sa thob nas//brtul shugs mnyam pa'i thabs kyis ni//bde chen ma skyes srasu brlabs//kun bzang thugs dang gnyi su med//phyi nang gsang ba'i rang bzhin gyis//snod bcud rgyud rnam dag pa ni//4nga
21a3(sha): ba chen po'i zhing khams nyid//ma lus dag pa nyid du gsal//sku gsung thugs la rabs gnas shing //cho ga rdzogs par shes pa yis//gnas dang longs spyod cing 'tshams par//bde ba chen pos 'phong med par//longs spyod ldan zhing dkur
21a4(sha): phub spangs//gnas dang longs spyod rgyan rnam dang //'byor pa ci 'dir bstan nas ni//gang yang 'grub ste gzhan du min//longs spyod dga' bas mi gsungs shing //yab yum 'dod pa lngar ldan pas//brtson pa'i tshul bzhin rabs sgrubs na//rdo lus
21a5(sha): dang rnam smin du ka/tha mal 'di yis 'grub par 'gyur//sa nas sar ni mngon 'phel ba//bla ma thabs dang gzung stob kyi//gong bzhin las bskal dag pa ni//myur du bar chad meng par 'grub//las dang dngos grub phra mo dag/chu rlabs lta bur rang
21a6(sha): snang ba//byin brlabs sngags dang ting 'dzin gyis//ji ltar 'dod par 'byung 'gyur te//nga rgyal zhen pa'i tshul dang bral//las dang dngos grub tha~Md la//zhen chags sbyor ba kun spangs la//bdag nyid chen po'i sbyor tshul gyis//'dir ni ci bde
21a7(sha): bar 'grub par bya//thabs dang shes rab ldan pa yis//mchags thabs kyi skyong nga ni//yongs pa med par rab 'grub ni/myur du 'grub ste gzhan du bam//rna 'byor grub po mo la//gnyis meng sbyor ba khyed pa tshe//gang du 'dzi ni grangs sbyi/
21b1(sha): /bas/ji lta spyad kyang 'grub par 'gyur//ma chags pa la chags spyad pas//chags pa nyid kyang 'grub par 'gyur te//spyong lam kun tu gnas pa yis//'di yis 'grub ste gzhan du min//bde chen phyag rgya'i rnal 'byor pa//lhun 'grub la
21b2(sha): gnas cho ga bzhin//dus dang rang rang skal ba bzhin//le lo the tsom med brtson pas//rab 'bring tha ma ji bzhin 'thob//thob bya kun gyis ngo bo ni//rang 'byung gsal ba'i snying po ste//gzung 'dzin tha dad kun gyis stong //byang chub na mkha'i rang
21b3(sha): bzhin no//thabs dang shes rab dbyer med pa'i//dkyil 'khor sna tshogs 'bar ba nyid do//bde chen sku gsung thugs nyid de//rang 'byung nyid las gzhan ma yin//thog med dus nas rab sbyangs pa'i//skal ldan dam las ma nyams pa'i//rang
21b4(sha): rang cho ga bzhin bsgrubs na//sangs rgyas tshe 'dis 'grub 'gyur na//rigs 'dzin la sogs smos ci dgos//rang rang goms pa'i thabs nyid do//mos dang 'tshams pa'i khyad par ltar//gzhan du cho ga nyams par 'gyur//dang ldan
21b5(sha): rang gi dam tshig 'ongs//bskal pa bye bas mi 'grub pa//nyi shu lnga ldan lhun 'grub pa//skye ba 'di la the tsom med//bya ba byed la ma chags shing //gnyis med sgyu mar gsal sgoms na//dka' thub gdung ba kun spangs pas//'di nyid la
21b6(sha): ni dga' bas 'grub//ces nyid la nyid bshod//rgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho'i rgyud las//tshogs kyi dkyil 'khor gsang ba'i le'u ste bcig pa'o///de nas bcom ldan de bzhin gshegs//rgyu 'phrul dra ba dgyes chen pos
21b7(sha): skal pa brgya stong dpag med du//snyen par dka' ba'i 'bras bu ste//rgyo 'phrul lam la bstan pa yis//tshe 'dis 'bras bu thob byor bshad //gsang sngags lam la gnas byas shing //zangs dang shes rab daphyir med pas//
22a1(sha): mtshan ma'i bya ba spangs pa yis/'di'am thar mar chos sku thob//gang du bden gnyis dbyer med pa'i//don la gnas pa'i rnamyor bas//byed pa sdom pa'i man ngag gis//byi nang ngas sdig bye ba'i rlung //snying po pas las phog
22a2(sha): pa yis//bde chen stong pa'i thig ler mnyam//snyig ma'i bag chags sangs gyur nas//bde chen ma mkha'i mtha' ltar rgyas//thabs dang shes rab nyi zla'i bcung //bsnyen bsgrub bzhi la sa goms pa yis//rang nyams rtags dang rna rlung gis//
22a3(sha): dug gis dug nad sel ba ltar//lhag gi skyes pa mngon du shar//de nyid bde la mi rtogs pa'i//bar chad spangs pas bsgrub pa yis//mun pa snang bas gsal ba ltar//ma lus bde ba chen por snang //bdag gzhan bral ba'i bde chen gyis//na mkha'
22a4(sha): 'od kyis gang ba ltar//dus gsum mangs non tan b+h+ya//gsal bar mngon du nan tan bya//mngon du gsal nas ma goms par//dbye ba med par gsal bar bya//bde chen na mkha'i mtha' mnyam pa//bskal pa rdul snyeng 'phel 'gri'i rnams//'gro
22a5(sha): 'od med pa'i ngo bo ni//bde ba chen po'i rdo rje 'chang //thog ma tha ma med pa ni//sangs rgyas kun gyis rang bzhin nyid//dang por rang bzhin skye med pa'i//mi dmigs pa las 'das pa yis//de bzhin nyid de ma bcos pa'i//rang bzhin nges par rtogs par bya//
22a6(sha): chos rnam rang bzhin dngos med pa'i//bdag gzhan dngos 'dzin zhen bral ba'i//de ni nyid ni ma bcos pa'i//rang bzhin ngang gis rnar sgom//dngos dang dngos med zhen bral ba'i//rang bzhin ye shes kun snang 'od//nam mkha'i lta bur phyogs med pa'i//rgyu ma

22b3(sha): lnga rdzogs pas//sku gsung thugs la nan tan bya//yes sems dpa' goms gyur nas//rigs bzhi sems dpa' yab yum dang //yab yum kun bzang goms par bcas//sbrul sku la sogs de bzhin shes//de nas rgyang grags dpag tshad dam///

22b6(sha): rung ro mchog ldan pa dang //glu gar rol me chog stabs bro//rgyan dang bgo ba phun sum tshogs//thabs dang shes rab nyi zlar ldan//glung tshig sgra dang ldan po yis//tshe la dbang bsgyur gnas thobs nas//goms nas phyag rgya'i rigasu nges//lhun

23a1(sha): @gzhan du bsod nams chung ba yis//sangs rgyas kun gyi sku gsung thugs//cig du sbyor bar ngo bor nga //yab yum sbyor ba cig du goms//phyogs bcu ngus pa ni rang ba nyid //ngo bo cog dang tshul cig dang /sku gsung thugs mtshas/
23a2(sha): bstan nas/byin brlabs ris grub rtsol bar mdzad//dpag tu mang pa'i rgyam brnasas//sku gsung thugasu snang ba tsas//mi ngang 'khobs rang snang do//ngo cog tu 'dus par 'gyur//de dag nyid kyis thob 'gyur bas//khyad par gnon nas yang ma yin/
23a3(sha): sangs rgyas zhing khams dpag med pa'i//rang 'byung byang chub snying po ste//'khor lo yi gi kun 'dus pas//thob par sna tshogs 'pho bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun sku gsung thugs//til 'bru rdul phran grangs mnyam pa//rab byung yis nyid kyi gzug

23a6(sha): rang bzhin ni//phyogs cha mtshan ma las 'das pa'i//rang rig brtos pa'i yul 'das pa//dngos med dngos po kun gyis gnas//'di nyid kun gyi yul min pas//rang bzhin thugs dang sgribs pa yis//la las chun longs ji bzhin du//bye brag yul
23a7(sha): du ma gyur te//tha mal zhen pa'i rang rgyud pa//'di ni 'jig rten phal pa nyid//rang bzhin ngo bo nyid la sogs pa//byed pa med cing gnyen po meng//gdod nas rang shugs kyis grub pa//ther zug rtag parang rgyud pa//gang yang med par blo bur du//snang
23b1(sha): ba 'jig rten 'di dag ni//sngon med phyis med gdod nas snang //phyis snang bar yang ji ltar du//la lar tha dad rtog pa ste//chos kyi bdag la dngos por 'dzin//la la bden gnyis rkyen byas nas//rten 'brel 'khor lo 'gog par byed//la lar tha dad mi gnas
23b2(sha): ste//rig pa'i rgyun ldan mi rtogs 'dod //gzhan dag rig pa rnams med de//na mkha'i dkyil ltar dmigs pa med//la lar rgyu ma'i don la gnas//la lar cha shas med pa'i//mtha'i spros pa rnar bcad//gang bsnyen po kun dang bral//gnyan pa'i don ni mtha'

24b2(sha): bapa go bzhi'i phyag rgya las//'byung bzhi rigs kyi yum du snang //phung po bde ba'i rgyas btab pas//dbang po yul sems rang bzhin thabs//kun bzang rigs pa'i dkyil 'khor la//lhan cig skyes pa'i rang bzhin gyis//rten dang rten pa'i mnyam sbyor bas/
24b3(sha): yeyis lnga ni rang gsal bas//bde ba'i yis dgyes pa chen//ma bsgoms bde ba'i longs spyod kyis//bde chen rgyu ma'i mtshan nyid bsal//bde ba'i rol mo bsam byas pa//gsang ba'i thig le ma skyes pa//kun gyi rang bzhin gnyisu med//phyogs
24b4(sha): bcu dus bzhi mtha' yas pa//rgyal ba'i che ba ma lus pa//lhan cig skyes par zhal mthong zhing //thig le rlung sogs dbang bsgyur bas//ma lus ye shes bde mchog yis//rang 'byung yis dkyil 'khor la//zag med dkyil 'khor rang gnas nyid//
24b5(sha): mthong goms rdzogs pas lhun gyis grub//ma spangs rang la lhun grub pas//ma lus dkyil 'khor rgyan du gnas//'byung 'jug med pa'i dkyil 'khor la//che ba'i yon tan lhun rdzogs pas//phyogs bcu na mkha'i mthar thug par//thugs rje'i
24b6(sha): dkyil 'khor phyogs snang //bye byed med pas tshul gcig pa//rang 'byung snying po dkyil 'khor mchog/thun mongs thun mongs ma yin pa//shes rab yum la mnyam sbyar nas//rang 'byung 'bras bu lhun gyi grub//bskal pa rdul snyed grangs 'das nas//
24b7(sha): mos dang sbyang ba'i rang bzhin gyis//gsang ba'i don la gang bzhugs pa//tshe 'di rgyal ba nyid du 'gyur//'das dang da ltar ma 'ong ba'i//rgyal ba'i sku gsung thugs kyi bcung //'dus pa'i gsang chen 'od yin te//lam gyi dam pa he lam yin//
25a1(sha): @'jig rten drug gi phyogs bcu na//rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor rdul phran snyed//bstan pa phyi nang bsang ba kun//rgyu 'phrul dra ba las 'bros te//khabs pa shes rab gnyis byed dang //bya ba dang spyod dang rnal 'byor dang //
25a2(sha): chen po zla med pa//ma lus 'dus shing 'byung ba'i bying //ngas gyu ba rlangs chen kun 'byung ba'i//rgyu phrul rgya mtsho'i rgya chen 'di//mos dang mthong du byis pa 'di//glags btsam gyi 'grub 'gyur na//rtog dang goms pa'i sbyor ba yis//'bras /
25a3(sha): thob pa smos ci gas//rang chung tsal lakho kun 'dum pa'o//rgyucan rgyu sa dra ba ste//b+h+yo na ces ni bdag pas mchog/ting po las kyi snying po mchi/ces nying sa nyid pabyung //rmyi byal rgya mtsha rgyalas/gsing ba dar bsnun
25a4(sha): skye bcu gsum pa'o///danas ma sa ngos po sa par gyi/rlung ba nan sgyur ci gru pa ste//gdusu gyur das pa bya sna tshogs byung//bras bum tsho 'phrul snang ba ma dang //ji snyed dang byung dkyil la ma
25a5(sha): tu byed med par rang snang bas//thig la chen po lhun gyigs pa'i//bdag gzhan phyer med de bzhin nyid //phyogs bcu rgya yongs ma chad pa'o//bde chen long skyid dpag tu med//zhing khams 'khor dang tshi ba nyid//snang 'khor lo rang snang nying //ma lus
25a6(sha): rang snang sku'i tshogs bas ma byas//rdul dang til gang shing //gang mos blo la snang byed pa//gdul bya me smai ba'i don du zad//gang du ma bcos de bzhin nyid//rang rig ngo bo gsalnga 'gyur ba//kun gyi rang bzhin rkyen bral ba//snga phyi med par kun
25a7(sha): mi gyi mchog/dus gsum phyogs 'du ma pa mas kun//dbye gyi rje ti sdong bas//phyogs meng byi rlangs ginyen pa bral//dus gsum 'dubral med par 'azhugs//mi byid ni rje rang ba zhin la//brjod bya'i ring bzhin skyon rdzogs pas
25b1(sha): dbyer med ngo bo kun snang bas//mi byed gsung ni kun gyi mchog/tshig dang yi ge kun bral ba//ma brjod dgongs pa brdas stan pas//snga phyi mtha' dbus chos thang //mnyam pa chen po'i tshul du ston//rang 'byung yis byin rlabs ni//gcig dang du ma lnga
25b2(sha): 'das pa'i nyan mkha'i dbyings ltar gnas pa yis//dgod pa chen por brtul ba nyid//rang rig skye ba med pa las//ji snyed chosu rang snang ba//mtha' dbus spros pa bcas pa yis//de bzhin nyid kyis gsung pa med//thug rje byin rlabs rang snang ba///
25b3(sha): sgra dang dbyangs la sogs pa rnams//mtshon pa thab ni dpag med du//thugs rje byin rlabs snang bar 'gyur//sang+yas kun gyi yon tan mchog/sku dang gsung thugs yon tan lnga//rgyal ba sngar ni snang mdzad//re re zhing khams gdan sogs snang //rang
25b4(sha): nga dmyi med byang chub sems//sangs rgyas kun gyi thugs cheno//rnam dag dri med 'od gsal las//mkhyen pa'i yis bzhir gsal nas//thad gdod nas dbyer med pas//gang dag de ltar mkhyen pa yis//bdag gzhan bya byed med pa yis//
25b5(sha): phrin las lngar ni snang bar 'gyur//steng 'og rten pa las 'byung ba'i//ye shes rten pa de bzhin nyid//sku dang gsung dang thugs 'khor lo//yon tan kun gyi las dang sbyar//dbyibs dang yi ge rang bzhin las//phrin las lder snang bar 'gyur//
25b6(sha): na mkha'i lta bu'i lnga rdzogs pa//zag med yon tan lhun rdzogs pa//byas pa med par rnams bzhi rdzogs//rang 'byung 'bras bu rten 'brel ba'i//ngo bo dbyer med de bzhin nyid//gzhon pa rlung gi stobs phra ba'i//thig le 'gre 'ong 'du 'gro
25b7(sha): med dbang sgyur phung po 'jigs meng bas//'byung ba ni 'du ba tshogs dang bral//rig pa rlung la dbang sgyur bas//sdug bsngal chu bas 'jigs dang grol//yis gting 'dzin las la 'grub//shes rab stobs sogs mnyam sbyar bas//
26a1(sha): 'phel 'grib thig le 'byung 'jug med//me dang lus dang bde ba dang /yis zag med gzugs ldan pas//ma lus zhing tshogs dag pa ste//bdung bani skyes chags par chang med //'byung ba ni rdzas ma la brten pas//ga
26a2(sha): pa'o bde min par chad med//rnam rtog 'gyu ba'i rlung dngas pas//bde chen na mkha' mthar ltar gnas//tha ma'i dbang la rang gnang bas//zhing khams rgya mtsho rdul snyed kyis//thabs dang shes rab mnyam sbyar nas//drin can nyid la dbang bskur ba//
26a3(sha): shes bya'i dkyil 'khor kun sad te//rmongs pa sangs ltar mngon sangs rgyas//de nas na mkha'i mtha' thug par//bde 'a chen po'i sku nyid dang //cho skye 'khor lo bskor mdzad cing //bde chen mi rtogs gsal ba'i thugs//zhing khams phyogs bcu
26a4(sha): rdul nyid//yon tan bsam byas mngon bstan nas//gdul bya'i don ni ji bzhin skyed pa//ma lus mdzad pas tshim par mdzad//lam dang mdzad pa zhing khams dag/sa dang khams dang mthun par snang //snod bcud rgyu zhen dri ma gsum//nyi zla sems
26a5(sha): 'thun 'gro 'ong gis//gnas 'dran pa'i lam nyid kyis//dag pa yab yum ye shes gsal//thabs dang shes rab dbyer med pa'i//'khor 'angs dbyer med rang bzhin la//'bru gsum 'chims nisaM rang zlog pa//thabs la ldan pas grar skyed/
26a6(sha): pas ta/ma skyed lhan cig skyed par gsal//rten can rlung dang thig le la//rang 'byung goms pa'i blo yis blta//rkyen dang bral bas mngon gyur na//sems kyi thig le 'tsher ba med//rang 'byung bde ba mngon sum ste//goms dang thugs rje rang
26a7(sha): snang ba'i//'dod yon long spyod sna tshogs la//lha mo ri cing stong gis bdag/grang meng 'khor lo tshul skor bas//ma g.yos bde ba kun khyab can//phung sogs rtogs pa thad kun//bde chen yis lnga ru gsal//de nyid bcu gsum sangs pa//yis
26b1(sha): dbyings nyid rang gnas pa'i//khams gnyis pa'i thabs las skyes//skyes pa nyid na bdag tu gsal//thabs la mkhas pa'i khyab par gyis//gang la 'du ba'i tshul snang ba/gang dag log pa yod med kyis//shes rab spyan gnyis yul
26b2(sha): cig kyang thabs lus phung po snang med bzhin//lkog med stobasu rdzogs po nyid//dang po 'jug pa'i rim nas//tha ma mthar byin rdzogs par du//phyi nang gsang sogs ji bzhin par//skyes 'gag gnas dmigs 'gro 'ong gos//g.yo sa
26b3(sha): skyes la mi spyod kyang //zab mo grags pas bskyed rims brtan//smon lam 'dod yon 'jug pa yang //gnyis med don la sdang ba med//gnas pa dag ni rnam gsum gyis//yid ches shes rab 'phel bar byed//rgyun du gnas pa'i spros bcas
26b4(sha): tshe ta mthar lo nyid la sbyor ba'o//bsgrub pa'i rgyu yis mi rtog pa//de sgoms phyi ma'i rigs las brtem//lhan 'grub phyi tshe yongasu rdzogs//thabs dang shes rab mnyam sbyor ba'o//bde ba thig le rdzogs skyes pa//rang bzhin med pa'i r+ya mar
26b5(sha): bgag/de nyid ma skyes dbyer med gnas//gnyis med rtogs las gang dmigs pa//'gro 'ong med pa'i 'bras bu 'char//smad pa'i tshig bral bsgo bskur med//rgyu rkyen 'bras bu bdag po dang //gnyis med don las mi g.yo zhing //'brel tshad
26b6(sha): 'khrug bsnol dka' rgyud yin//zhes nyid la nyid bshad//rgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho'i rgyud las//dngos po lnga bsten la phab pa'i le'u ste bcu pa'o///de nas bcom ldan 'das dgyes chen po//'dul ba'i bsam pa la dgongs
26b7(sha): nas /dkyil 'khor rdul snyeng bcas pa dag//khro bo dpag tu meng par gyur//gang phyir gdul bya dang ga ming dgi/khro bas 'dul ba'i dor bye'o//kha cig sdang sems ldan pa la//sku gsum thug su ldan pa yis//thabs mkhas skyon dang bral barmi/
27a1(sha): @#zhe sdang khros pas 'dul bar mdzad//kha cig chags shing zhen pa ste//thabs dang shes rab dbyer med pa'i//'dod chags rmongs par thim mdzad nas//chags pa khros pas 'dul bar mdzad//la lar lha dang tsha dang dngangs//
27a2(sha): ting 'dzin zhing khams cho 'phrul gyis//'pham zhing nga rgyal zil mnan sa//'pham zhing nga rgyal zil mnan sa//log pa'i ri bo ltas mthong nas//log pa'i ri bo tshar gcod cing //thog ma tha ma'i dus med pa//rang 'byung rgyu 'phrul 'gag pa med
27a3(sha): thabs mkhas phan pa'i rang bzhin gyis//'jig rten gom gsum bya ba la//'jig dang re dog med pa yis//mi bya ba ni gang yang med//ting 'dzin bye ba sa ya dag/dpag med che bar kun sprul nas//mtshan ma'i che ba zil mnan nas//thabs
27a4(sha): mkhas yid 'phams 'phrog par mdzad//stong gsum khrag dang dur khrod dang //dga' dang mi dga' 'jigs byed pa'i//ma lus dur khrod ro gling du//stobs kyi gdugs pa zil gyis gnon//'jig rten khams ni ma lus pa//g.yo zhing 'bru
27a5(sha): dang lhag pa yis//gdugs pas spa kong 'jig mdzad nas//skal pa me ltar rab 'bar zhing //'jig byed chen po brgyan gyis bzhugs//'di ltar ma lus zil gnon pas//nga rgyal khros pas 'dul bar mdzad//de nas sangs rgyas thad ni//ma
27a6(sha): lus pa'i bdag nyid des//ma lus byin rlabs gsal ba'i phyir//gnyis med sbyor ba'i thig le yis//ma lus khyab cing gang mdzad nas//thig le rdul phran 'phros pa yis//dag pa bde chen lhun grub par//thad byin rlabs gsal ba yis//
27a7(sha): ma lus dkyil 'khor chen po gnas//dpal ldan 'dus pa'i ngo bo la//rigs lnga'i tshul ni rdor 'phrul//la lar rjes sbyor mdzad cing //la lar kun gyis raM ba skong //la lar ma chags skyon bral nyid//la lar cho 'phrul mtha' yas ston//yaMs pra
27b1(sha): /rags gnyi su med//bde chen rigs pa'i stobs 'phel bas//shes brgyad ni rang ldog pas//bde chen rigs pa kha bltas nas//rang rang las dang skal bar gnas//bde chen rol pa rang snang bas//yul rnam gnyis med 'dres pa nyid//rdo bde
27b2(sha): chen 'bra men nyid//yul sems byi nang thig ler gsal//'khor 'das gnyis med mchin par mdzad//thabs tshan gsang ba la rten nas//rang 'byung gsal ba'i nyi zla 'od//dbab dang gzung dang gnas bcing zhing //rang rang gnasu slar son pas/
27b3(sha): mi shes yul las 'das pa yis//bde chen gsang ba'i sku gsung thugs//ma lus dkyil 'khor chen por bsal//gang du yid las 'das pa yis//bde chen ngal mtha' dbusu med//rten med thig le 'gro 'ongad spangs//rang 'byung gnas pa'i
27b4(sha): snying por thim//phyi nang yul sems kun bral ba'i//rang 'byung rgyu 'phrul dra ba ste//thabs chen mi dmigs chos kyi dbyings//rgyu 'phrul dra ba'i dkyil 'khor//rang bzhin thabs dang shes rab las//bde chen rang bzhin gsal bar 'char//
27b5(sha): phyogs bcu 'jig rten snod bcud bcas//bde chen thig le dkyil 'khor snang //rje su 'dzin pa bzhi ldan zhing //yul sems lus dang dus bzhir bcas//rdo rje 'dzin par byin rlabs shing //gsang ba'i don du gnyisu med//rdo rjer nyi ma
27b6(sha): nub nas ni//rang 'byung dpa'i'i zla ba nyid//sgrib byed med pa'i tshul du 'char//ma lus dbyer med tshul du gnas//rgyu 'phrul dra ba mngon rdzogs pa//thog mtha' med pa'i rje sems//snying po gnyis med bde chen po//rang 'byung gnyis med
27b7(sha): du gsal//rang 'byung gnyis ming thig lo yiso//ma lus stongs par mdzad pa yas//rdo rje khro bo'i phrin las te//za byed ces kyang bshangad pa yin//gang du rgyu ba kun zang pa'i//phyi ning gsang ba'i thig le ni//rten dang byin par bcas pa ni//
28a1(sha): zang med pa de chen dang du thim//rang rig ngos dang ngos bral ba'o//mtha' dbus bzung 'dzir yul 'das pas//skye med bde nam chos kyi dbyings//bden par dbyer med don dugs la//tshig dang yi ge las 'das pa'i//de chen ldan/
28a2(sha): tsag skyes pa ni//gang gis mtshon pa las 'das kyang //dbang dang man ngag stobs las snang //thabs dang shes rab 'brel pa yis//rang 'byung ye shes pade tshan ni//rang bzhin sbyangs las snang gyur pa'i//skal ldan rnam kyi spyod yul te//las nga
28a3(sha): /sgro bkur dbang byas shing //rmongs sogs dman pa'i yul ma yin//gsang ba thabs chen snying po'i rgyud//sangs rgyas kun gyi gsang chen 'di//bde chen na mkha'i kun gsal nas//yi ni dang ma yin yul las 'das//phyogs bcu'i rdul rnyed
28a4(sha): sangs rgyas kyi //thugs kyi thugs te thugs kyi mchog/ma lus gsang mchog rgyan gyis mdzes//de ltas rgyal sras de bzhin gshegs//ces nyid gsungaso//rgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho'i rgyud las//rdo khro bo'i rang bzhin mngon du phyung ba'i

28a6(sha): ba yisatam lus ye shes byin brlabs nas//bde ba chen por gsal bar byed//gang du goms bstan pa yis//thabs dang shes rab las byung ba'i//bde ba'i thig le thugs pa med//ye sa lnga zhes gang bshad ba//rgyal ba kun gyis
28a7(sha): snying po lnga//sa bon yi se oM zhes bshad//thog mtha' med pa'i de bzhin nyid//gnyis med mi phyed 'jims pa ni//thugs kyi rdo gang yin pa//badz+ra zhes ni brjod pa yin//ma bcos de bzhin ye shes ni//re dog 'gro 'ong med son pa//
28b1(sha): /chos kyi dbyings kyi sku las gyur//ta thA ga ta zhes brjod do//ma lus rang 'byung rin po che//nam mkha' lta bu zad med cing //dbang bskur thog med yis nI//rat+na zhes pa brjod pa yin//'khor ba'i bag chags spongs pa ni//bde ba chen po
28b2(sha): skyon med pa//'dod chags bya bral bcom pa nyid//pad+ma nyid ni yin par bshad//thog med brtson 'grus pha rol byin//bya byed phrin las mthar phyin pa//'gro 'ong med pa'i thabs nyid ni//karma las kyi rang bzhin nyid//gnyisu med
28b3(sha): po'i yaMs te//don grub lngar ni bsngags pa yin//mahA chen po lnga nyid de//thig le chen po las byung ba//sku gsum yon tan 'phrin las thugs//ma lus chen po lnga nyid do//nyon mongs lnga 'joms khro bo ni//he ru ka zhes brjod
28b4(sha): pa ste//yis 'bar ba'i mtshan nyid ni//khrag thung lngar ni bshad pa yin//thabs kyi yulnga bcos pa ni//ma hA tsaN+Da zhes brjod de//khro bas khro ba spangs pa ni//gtum po chen po de nyid do//ma rungs pa dang bdud bzhi dang /dam tshig
28b5(sha): sdang ba thad ni//bdag dang gzhan 'tshe bar gcod pa//thad sarba duSh+TAn nyid//gsung ba'i thabs chen ma rungs pa//gang gris 'jom sa pa ta kra ste//gnyen pos 'joms pa ha na nyid//gang gis nga ha 'joms pa nyid//bag chags zag
28b6(sha): pe pa tsa nyid//hUM ni thugs kyi rang bzhin te/rbad dang sgrol dang drag po'i sgo//'di ni pan zhes bskur bar bshang //ye shes rang bzhin skye med pa//zab mo thig le 'dus ma byas//oM zhes phra mo yul lngar brjod//rig lnga phra bar gang
28b7(sha): brjong pa//badz+ra la sogs bshad pa rdze//ma rig spyod brtan mo nus pa//yumachen lnga zhes bshad pa'o//yul dang shes pa gnyis med pa//bab mo thog le 'dus ma byas//kun gyi 'byung gnas byi me 'jug go//gang ro dang byug
29a1(sha): @#lngar bshad pa'o//gang ni sa gang 'dzin mi gtong ba//de nyid d+ha ra gzungs mar bshad//ha ni mtshan med dus ma byas//steng brgyan thugs rje chen po nyid//rang bzhin chags pa bde chen nyid//gsung rab rgya mtsho sgra mtha

29b1(sha): nas ba chu chub tsu ba las 'thung //khubs dang shes rab la dril ba las//byug rgya bzhi dang ldan pa gnas//byon las byed brams ste//las kyi mkha 'gro thog le cags/rgyu las kyi tshig pa /6b+h+ya \u0f7f//spyan pag
