29b6(sha): gsum rgyan khams kyis mdzes//rnar thar pa sgo bzhi la//dur khrod rgyan chas rta babs bstan//thod 'phreng bla re nyi zla'i rgyan//'jigs shing mdzes pa sna tshogs ldan//'khor ba'i rmang 'byung 'bar ba'i gnas//khrag 'thung gzhugs pa'i gzhal yas/
29b7(sha): khang ta mkha 'gro sna tshogs 'chag pa'i sa//rol pa'i lha mo sna tshogs kyang //lha dang mdzes tshul sna tshogs kyis//'khor lo tshul du gnas pa la//mdog dang rang ches lugs bzhin du//dga' dang mdzes dang chags pa dang /ro rang sdig par byed pa dang /
30a1(sha): /rnams dang las la brtson la sogs//'khor lo tshul du bskur te gnas//zhing khams bye ba rdul mnyams pa//khrag 'thungs so sor 'khor dang banas//mnyam pas mnyam pa'i chub ston zhing //sku dang gsung thugs phyer med pa'i//8/
30a2(sha): bzhin nyid du dbyer med thim//rgyud chen sgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho yis//che ba'i yon tan bsam yas pa//nam mkha'i khams las ma 'das pa'i//'khor ba'i dkyil 'khor sna tshogs snang //de nyid rang rig rang snang bas//sku gsung thugs kyi cho ga yis//dbang
30a3(sha): bskur byin rlabs kun ldan nas//lhag pa'i ting 'dzin bstan par goms//mtshan ma'i chos kun spangs pa'i//ba ga gsang ba'i gzhal yas khang //thig chen gsang ba'i grong khyer te/thabs dang shes rab dbyer med pa'i//yon tan dpag med rdzog
30a4(sha): par snang //chags dang rmongs dang g.yeng ba dang //btang snyoms tha mal la sogs pa//'jig dang bag tsha rnam par gcod//thig le rdul phran grangs mnyam pa//bde ba chen por ro gcig cing //mu mtha' med pa'i gnasu 'jug/chags sdang la rten grong khyer//
30a5(sha): dang /'jigs dang bag tsha'i grong khyer dang //thabs dang ldan pa'i khyad par gyis//de nyid rnam par spyod par byed//dbyibs kyi ba ga brtan pa'i gnas//thig le spros med rang 'byung ba'i//bde ba chen po thog mtha' med//bdag po gnas dang dbyer med
30a6(sha): pa'o//thig le ma skyes spros med pa//bden pa bzhir ni rab 'byar bas//phyi nang long spyod kun tu 'phel//dga' bzhi rim pa brtags pa yis//byang chub sems kyis thig le gnyis//bde chen sna tshogs dga' bas brgyan//thig le pra rogs
30a7(sha): snang ba kun//'babo zhon bsnol ba'i thabs nyid kyis//chags sogs lnga po zo li gnon pos//dag pa lngar ni rjer gnas//thig le ting 'dzin rong bcas dang //gnas dang mnyam kyis khyad par gyis//rang rang las dang 'thun pa'o//mdog dang rim par gyur
30b1(sha): pa yin te bde chen rna thar pa la//mi gnas ye shes drug ldan pas//phung po la sogs par chod bral//thabs kyis bde chen lus brgyas pas//g.yo ba sna tshogs bcas pa yis/thig le stong par bcas pa yis//'jig dang sdigs dang dga' ba yis/sgra
30b2(sha): rnam sna tshogs ston par byed//chags pas thig le phreng bcas te//rdo rje snang ngas khyab par byed//rang lus thabs dang bcas pa yis//thig le stong par bcas pa yis//gcod dang 'joms sogs sna tshogs pas/bde ba'i ro ni sna tshogs lhan//
30b3(sha): gnyis med ro ni thig ler 'brel//rtswa bcud shes par bcas pa yis//thig le'i phyag rgya spyir bcas pas//bde chen gsang ba'i thig le la//dgug cing sgrol stim la sogs pa//bya ba'i las la dbang thob 'gyur//steng 'og thabs las 'byung //
30b4(sha): ba yis//gsang ba'i thig le chen ni//yon tan rdzogs pa'i bdag nyid de//sna tshogs ro dang bcas pa yis//nyi zla 'khor lo ge sar bcas//gsang ba'i lus la rtag tu gnas//bye phrag brgya bcu kun 'dus pa'i//phyogs bzhi steng 'og bcas
30b5(sha): pa yis//bde chen 'bar bas khyab gyur cing //phyi nang bcud kyis gang bar byed//gsang ba'i de nyid nges pa yis//rgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho'i gsang ba'i don//ma lus khyab par gsal gyur cing //dag pa rnams la mngon gyur kyang //dbang dang man ngag
30b6(sha): bral ba yis//rang bzhin skal pa dman pa la//thabs kyis thig pa mi snang ste//rigs chang 'khor ba'i rigs 'chang yin//bde chen gsang ba'i snying po 'di//ma lus rdzogs pa'i ngo bo ste//rang bzhin thabs las mngon gyur pas//thabs chen
30b7(sha): lhun grub mngon du gyur//rdo rjer thig le son pa yis//gsang ba las kyi mi long la//ji bzhin mngon du byas pa yis//rdo rje rigs pa'i lung thob cing //dbang bskur phyag rgya lhun gyis grub//ces gsung ba'i tshig tu'o//rgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho'i rgyang las//
31a1(sha): lhun gyis grub pa'i dkyil 'khor bstan pa'i le'u ste bcu bdun pa'o///de nas bcom ldan dgyes chen pos//ma lus mchod pa'i sprin dgong te//byi nang gsang ba sna tshogs pa'i//dgyes pa'i mchod
31a2(sha): pa bstan par mdzad//ji srid na mkha'i mthar thug pa'i//sangs rgyas rdul snyed mtha' yas pa//dbang po'i grong khyer mnyes mdzad pa'i//rang gi lag cha mdzes ldan pa'i//lha mo'i tshogs ni bsam byas pa//dgyes pa'i mnyes tshul sna tshogs kyis//
31a3(sha): 'khor lo bdag po mnyes par mdzad//ma lus long spyong tshogs mchog mnyes//ma rtogs pa dang log par rtogs//phyogs rtogs yang dag nyid ma rtog/dbyer med dag mnyam tshul brtags nas//lhun gyis grub pa bzhid du sgo mi//thabs dang tIng
31a4(sha): 'dzin goms pa yis//gnas gdan drag pos tshar bcad nas//tshogs gnyis rdzogs pa'i rgyal sras la//skyes shi med par lhun gyi grub//ma lus thabs dang shes rab du//rtogs dang goms pa sbyor ba yis//bde ba chen po'i thig le yis
31a5(sha): thabs dang sbyor bas mnyes par bya//ma lus rigs lngar mnyam sbyar nas//mi 'chi lnga dang shA lde dag/'jigs pa med pa'i dpa' bo yis//mchod cing long spyod chen por bya//long skyong lnga dang mnyam sbyor bas//phyi nang gsang bar mnyam sbyor zhing //
31a6(sha): bde ba chen po gnyis med pas//dkyil 'khor lnga dang ma nyams par gnas//phyogs dus rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor che//ma lus cig tu mnyam nyid pas//gnyis med rang 'byung ngo bo nyid//rtogs goms gnas pas lhun gyis 'grub//'dres dang ma
31a7(sha): rjes mnyam gnas pa//thabs dang shes rab thig le nyid//rol pa'i thabs las rang 'byung ba//bde chen gar gyis snying pa mchog/ma lus rang 'byung snying po la//'dod ba'i stobs kyis nyer rdzogs shing //thabs kyi sbyor ba gang gnas pa//yon
31b1(sha): tan lhun grub snying por gnas//sangs rgyas kun gyis rmang 'byung ba//rang 'byung thabs kyis byin brlabs shing //gsang ba'i me long gsal gnas na//lhun grub 'bras bu gzhan las min//phyogs bcu rdul snyed grangs ma nyams pa/
31b2(sha): 'bras bu che ba lhun gyis rdzogs ngag/ma lus bdag nyid lhun rdzogs pas//yon tan gzhan dag smos ci dgos//thabs dang shes rab gnyis med pa'i//ma lus kun la rgyas btab cing //thabs ldan rnams la snang gyur kyang /kun la
31b3(sha): gsal bar snang ma yin//gsang ba thig le chen po ni//yon tan stobs kyi bdag nyid de//rtogs goms mnyes pas lhun grub cing //log dang le lo stobs kyis min//rgyu 'phrul dra ba mngon rdzogs pa'i//thig le rdzogs pa'i thabs
31b4(sha): kyis bskul//gsang ba'i rigs pa thabs kyis mnan//ro gcig bde ba chen por gsal//rnal 'byor thabs dang shes rab kyis//ma lus bde ba che rtogs shing //thig le gseng ba'i ros khyab na//bya spyod dkyil 'khor gsal bar
31b5(sha): 'gyur//ma lus ma bcos ma nyams sbyor zhing //dkyil 'khor gsum la goms pa dang //ngag dang rgyal bar ma nyams sbyor bas//ma lus mchod pa'i cho gar gnas//gang du bskul dang chags pa dang //dga' dang bde bas brgyan pa yis//
31b6(sha): gsang ba'i rol mos gang bskul ba//sangs rgyas kun gyis bde chen ni//phyi nang yul sems ma lus kun//bdag nyid chen po ros gang zhing //bde chen rdo gnyisu med//de nyid zag pa med par gnas//gnyis med 'dres pa'i rang bzhin la//
31b7(sha): gsang ba'i yis rang bzhin gsol//thabs tshon gsang ba las byung ba'i//rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor ma lus rdzogs//ma lus bdag tu dbyer med pas//thaMd gsang ba lhun gyis grub//zhes nyid la nyid gsung so//rgya 'phrul rgya mtsho'i rgyung las//
32a1(sha): gnyis pa chen pos byin gyis brlabs pa'i le'u ste bco brgyad pa'o///de nas bcom ldan dgyes chen pos//ma lus bdag nyid lhun grub pas//bdag nyid bde bar byin rlabs pa//dam tshig
32a2(sha): mchog gis bshad par bya//gang du don la gnas pa dag//bdag gzhan don ni lhun grub phyir//rgyal ba kun gyis byin rlabs pa'i//gang mi 'dar ba dam tshig mchog/snying po phyag rgya sngags rig rnams//gsal la yid gnyis med pa yis//
32a3(sha): rje sku gsung thugs sbyor bas//gang yang nyams med 'bras bur 'grub//don dam snying por gnyis med cing //thabs kyi don la rab nges na//las dang spyod pa gang yang rung //ma bsags mi tshogs gyur te//'dul sdom dam tshig ma lus pa//dam tshig
32a4(sha): chen po'i rang bzhin du///ma byas rang gi ngo bor rdzogs//'dul sdom lta dpyod rnam par dag/blang dor med par byin rlabs shing //ma lus 'dus par rdzogs te gnas//sangs rgyas kun gyis byin rlabs pa'i//thabs chen gsang ba'i rang bzhin la//
32a5(sha): mtha' dbus yod med las 'das pa'i//ma lus rgyu mar mnyam sbyor bas//rlung sems stong par ye dag pas//skye shi 'gyur med na mkha'i ngang //grub pas bdag gzhan med snang bas//dngos gcig bsgral bas ma lus bsgral//ma lus
32a6(sha): ngag dang mnyam sbyor bas//thig le chen po gnyisu med//gsang po gsang chen las 'byung bas//ma lus gnyis med dkyil 'khor mchog/ma lus dbyer med tshul gcig pas//gnyis dang yul du mi 'byed//rang 'byung longs sbyong gsal
32a7(sha): rtogs pas//blangs pas yon tan phun sum tshogs//chos rnams sgra tshigs bral ba yis//de bzhin nyid kyis rnams 'phrul pa//sgra brnyan brag cer byin brlangs pas//ma lus bden rdzun chog tu smra//gang du rtogs dang gnyer ba'i//rang sems
32b1(sha): rje byin rlabs pas//gang du bde las 'da' byed ltung //de bas sbyangs par su zhig sems//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam shing //dri med rgyud la gang 'joms pa//spangs pas 'khor ba'i tha mar te//rmi lam du yang spangs par min//las dang nyon mo/
32b2(sha): bdag byed cing ka ye shes dkyil 'khor 'grub byed pa//snying po sngags kyi dam tshogs la//skad cig tsam las 'das mi rung //rgyal ba kun gyis thugs 'jug cing //ye shes snying po rgyas 'debs pa//phyag rgya thugs kyi rang bzhin las//
32b3(sha): 'das na rgyal ba nyid kyang lhung //dam pa'i grong khyer 'jug pa'i grog//'od dang snying po ji bzhin du//thog ma tha mar mi byed pa//'bral dang dbyer bar bya ba min//sangs rgyas kun gyis gsang ba'i don//gnyer gtang bas kyang gzhan la min//gnyis
32b4(sha): ga ltung 'jig 'byung bas na//dam pa nyid la nges par bya//'joms pa chen po zhe sdang che//gtum pa chen po sreg pa nyid//'dod chags chen po bde ba ches//thabs chen thar pa chen po nyid//gti mug chen po rmongs pa che//gnyis med bdag nyid
32b5(sha): gsang ba che//nga rgyal chen po gnon gsang che//che ba chen por rang bzhin gnas//mi mthun spangs pa'i rang bzhin bzhi//theg pa chen po dam tshig mchog/dam pa'i rang bzhin dam pa'i mchog/gnas na dam par lhun grub cing //'das na dam pa
32b6(sha): tshig par 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun gyis byin brlabs pa'i//brten par bya ba'i dam tshig lnga ka/byin brlabs bar chad sel bar byed//dngos grub bsgrub pa'i dam tshig ste//rin chen dag pa'i chu nyid dang //dkar chen dmar chen 'jig pa'i rdzas//
32b7(sha): dpa' bo re dog med par sbyad//dpa' bo re dog med par spyad//gsang ba'i thig le dbyer med pas//thabs dang brtul zhugs ldan pas blangs//lhan cig gnas pa'i bde chen po//sangs rgyas kun gyis rang bzhin nyid//thabs dang nyi zlas
33a1(sha): @#/ros khyab cing //bde ba chen pos byin rlabs pas//rlung dang phyag rgya gang sngags pa//thugs kyi phyag rgya ye shes che//lus sems bde chen dbyer med pa'i//mi 'bral gzungasu bshad pa'o//rmongs dang rang bzhin ngan pa dang /
33a2(sha): skal med dbang dang bral ba la//thabs kyi mngon du mi bstan te/thig le 'byung 'jug khar 'byung min//steng 'og thig le 'tshams sbyar nas//thabs sten dpa'a'o 'dod chags kyis//bskal chags yul dang zhen pa dang /lha ngan bdag 'dzin rnam
33a3(sha): par 'joms//gnyis med rig pa rab gsal bas//ma lus bde chen ngo bor gnas//rang bzhin zhes bya zhen pa dag/seng ge lta bus zol gyis mnan//dman dang chags bral dka' thub can//zad dang ma smin bde chen bral//gang du thabs dang bral
33a4(sha): ba na/spangs zhing 'jug par bya ba min//lus las 'byung ba'i bcud lnga ni//rna khyor mnyam la rtag gnas pa'i//dam tshig 'tsho ba'i longs spyod pas//nam yang dor bar bya ba min//ma lus 'dus pa'i bcud yin pas//snying po lnga zhes de la bya//
33a5(sha): rtsa bcu nga shes pa'i ngo bo nyid//rang 'byung dag par gsal rtag shing //gnyis med sbyor bas goms pa yis//thad bde ba chen por mnyam//thun mongs thun mongs ma yin pa'i//'da' ka dam tshig rang bzhin te//rtsa ba yan lag bcas pa la//'das
33a6(sha): ba gang yin dmyal bar 'gro//dbyer med mnyam pa'i rang bzhin la//man ngag thabs kyis mnyam sbyar nas//ma lus bde ba phun sum 'tshogs//bdag las 'byung gi gzhan las min//gang du rtsa ba kun mnyam pa//yan lag bcas pa
33a7(sha): rang bzhin 'das//kun gyis spangs chang gnas yin phyir//'grogs dang phrad nas ltung bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun gyis byin brlabs pa//rtsa ba dam tshig gang 'das pa//rgyal ba 'khor bcas bkas bcang nas//thar med sdug bsngal
33b1(sha): gan su ltung //yan lag ngam tshag chang ba //las nas dngos grub mi 'byung zhing //theg chem rtswa spyo spangs pa pas//ngan 'gror 'gro ba'i gnas lhung //sangs rgyas kun gyis su gsung thugs//rjo sa tshig can po ste//malus gsang r+tha/

33b4(sha): nyid de//phrin las tshul ni yang dag bshad//rang bzhin thabs dang thugs rje nyid//phrin las rgya chen gang byung ba//de nyid rgyu 'phrul rgya mtsho ste//sangs rgyas kun gyis ngo bor 'dod//e ma ngo mtshar rmad kyis chos//rang 'byung ye shes dkyi lor du//
33b5(sha): snang sring rang bzhin ma bcos pa//bde ba chen po'i ngo bor zhi//ma bsngags lhun rdzogs dkyil 'khor du//che ba'i yon tan rang snang ba//'phel 'grib med par rang gsal ba//bsam gyis mi khyab mtha' yas mchog/dag pa'i dkyil lo//
33b6(sha): duta spyod yul bag chags ma lus pa//gsung mchog ma skyes byin brlabs kyis//rig pa ma bcos dang du 'dus//rang rig yis rgyal po yis//skye med rang bzhin dang du nyid//mtshan ma bag chags bcom nas ni//stong pa chen po'i ngang /
33b7(sha): du gnas//rang snying d+harma tsak+Sha la//yis bzhi sogs gnas po'i mthus//rang bzhin nus mthu bzhir snang ba//de ngag ma bcos shes par bya//shes pa mnyes pa la gnas na//mtshan ma'i rtog thob gyur na//rang rang 'thang ba'i sa phyog/
34a1(sha): @su rka las rnams brtsams nas 'grub par 'gyur//rdza gi dkyil 'khor rim gsum du//mtshan ma rdzas dang ldan pa la//dang ba dang ldan phur btab nas/ma lus sbyangs phyir mchod sbyin bya//ma'i dkyil 'khor rim gsum
34a2(sha): duha mtshan ma rdzas dang ldan pa la//dga' bas nga rgyal bur btab nA//yon tan 'phel phyir mchod sbyin bya//ya'i tshogs kyi dkyil 'khor du//rang gi mtshan ma rdzus ldan la//chags pa dbang byed phyar btab nas//mi mthun 'du byar mchod sbyin

40b7(sha): gsang ba'i yis rin po che 'ong 'bar ba'i gzhal yas khang //rgya ma mkha'i dbyings ltar yongasu ma tshang ba//yis yon tan kun tu rgyas pa'i dbyibs dang ldan pa//gsang ba lhag pa'i blo 'bur gyis mdzes pa//rtse mo cig tu 'dus pa'i dbyor
41a1(sha): @#med pa'i ro dang ldan pas thig le zlum pa//yis bsam gyis mi khyab pa//yis rin po che'i dbyibs dang kha dog la sogs pa/rnam pa tha dad pa'i bye brag dang /khyad par du gyur pa/dpag pa/tshang du dpag pa/yis rin po
41a2(sha): che'i phreng ba dang //chun 'phyang dang /shar bu'i brgyan dang //gzugs sna tshogs dang //sgra sna tshogs dang //dri sna tshogs dang /r-i sna tshogs dang /reg bya sna tshogs pas//'khrig por byung la//mi sgrib par rang rig par gsal ba'i rgyan bsam gyis mi

41b1(sha): /dbyings dang /mnyen pa'i dbyings dang /dro ba'i dbyings dang /bskyod pa'i dbyings la sogs pa/btsun mo'i dbyings dang gnyi su med par chos kyi byings kum tu mtha' yas par khyab pa ni/'di lta ste/dper na til gyi gang bu bzhin/gang nas khyab par

41b3(sha): dang /byang chub chen po rdo rje mthong byed dang /byang chub chen po rdo thos byed dang /byang chub chen po rdo snoms byed dang /byang chub chen po rdo rje myongs byed dang /btsun mo 'das pa dang /da ltar dang /'byung ba dang /ma byon pa'i tshogs dang /'joms pa chen

42a1(sha): pa'i//bde ba chen po'i gsang ba yis/bde mchog dbyings dbang bsku ro//zhes ched du gle //de nas yang bcom ldan 'das dpal yis rgyal po nyid//yis lnga'i ngo bo nyid sku dang gsung dang thugs las byu //e ma'o ngo mtshan
42a2(sha): /rmad kyi chos//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kun gyis gsang //skye ba med la tha~Md skyes//skyes pa nyid na skye ba med/e ma'o ngo mtshar rmad kyi chos/rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kun gyi gsang /'gag pa med las thang 'gag/'gag pa nyid na 'gag

42a7(sha): shes rab dbyings kyi 'ong //yis rdzogs pa'i dben la phog/'jug ldog bstan pa'i gar thabs kyis//slar yang bde tshon dbyangs la gsel//rang rig yis mchog tu skyed /zab mo'i dbang la kun rdzogs pa//gdams pa lung gi gsal
42a8(sha): thig le rim gsuM bskyed pa'i tshul/rdzogs chen dang la rim pa med/gnas bzhi cig tu 'khyil ba wa//yas lnga ru gsal bar 'char//gsang ba'o rtog pa ma lus pa/
42b1(sha): bar shes//bzhi bcu rtsa ldi'i rang bzhin nyid//phyi nang gsang ba'i tshul du bzhugs//sems lnga'i rims pa mtshams sbyar zhing //snyen bsgrub bzhi la gnas bcas pas//sa bcu lam lnga'i bag chags sbyang //bde chen rigs

47a1(sha): @/rgyas btab pas//zag med yis khyad par 'phags/me rlung 'phrul gyis lcags kyu'i//bskyod pa rlung gi phyag rgya ris//les sa bon zhu ba yis//dbab pas bde ba thim par 'gyur//thugs kyi dkyil 'khor
47a2(sha): /dam pa de//rab tu 'phra ba'i sprul pa 'am//mi g.yo gzung ba'i rlung stobs kyis//gzung dang skyil dang drangs pa las//bde ba'i rtags rnams 'gyur te//gar thabs chung la dbang bsgyur zhing //byed pa sdom pa'i man ngag gis//bde ba'i sa bon
47a3(sha): dbang bsgyur na//rang byung ye shes 'bar ba 'byung //thabs kyi zani dang ma zin las//mya ngan 'ongs dang 'khor bar snang //zab mo dbang gsum thob bya zhing //bla ma'i man ngag ldan gyur nas//rgya ba'i rlung la dbang thob nas//'khor ba sang+yas
47a4(sha): nyid du sbyor//ma ni dkyil 'khor gsang ba'i don//ma nu 'khor ba rnyog pa'i yid //tra zhes bya ba skyob pa nyid//zag med yis dkyil 'khor gnas//rgyu ba'i rlung la dbang thob nas//'khor ba snyoms 'jug bsgoms pas snyim pa los//
47a5(sha): yis lnga'i rgyas btab ste//sku gsung thugasu gsal bar snang //skyob pa bde mchog mtshan nyid kyis//ma lus dkyil 'khor nyid sbyor//dbang thob rig pa 'dzin pa yis//spyod yul phyi nang ma lus pa//thabs dang shes rab gnyis med
47a6(sha): pa'i//bde chen gsang ba'i rgyas bta go/khyab dang gang ba'i bdag nyid can//yis chen po mu mtha' med//rgyu ba rlung gis stobs dang bral//rnam rtog ye shes nyid du 'gyur//zhag ni sum cu phrag drug gam//lo gcig zla bzhi tshun
47a7(sha): chang tu bde ba'i las 'phel bar 'gyur//sang+yas pa la the tsom med/ces rjes gsang ba'i tshig du'o//sgyu 'phrul lo shes snying po'i rgyud las/gsang sngags kyi rig pa byin gyis brlabs pa'i le'u ste bdun pa'o//
47b1(sha): de nas de bzhin gshegs pa dgyes pa chen po nyid kyis/phyag rgya chen po rang snang ba'i tingange 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas ched du brjod pa 'di brjod//thad rang rig byang chub sems//yis snying po gnyisu med//thabs dang shes rab gnyis
47b2(sha): med pa'i//'jug pa'i thabs mkhas 'byor pa yis//phyag rgya'i tshogs chen gsal bar ldan//'khor lo 'dus po'i snying po la//rgyal ba'i yis rdzogs par ldan//dbang po yul du gsang ba'i sems//sku gsung thugs kyi rang bzhin la//
47b3(sha): yon tan phrin las kun tu rdzogs//gdam dag steng 'og las byung ba'i//thabs chen thabs las byung ba'i//'phel ba'i rang 'gi bsam mi khyab//sgo sku gnas pa'i 'dzin pa dag/rjes dran yan lag bzhi dang ldan//zhal dang pad+ma dbang po
47b4(sha): dag//gsang ba'i ro dag ldan 'gyur zhing //goms pa'i stobs las byung ba'i don//sgyu 'phrul dra bar nges par brjod//yis snying po byang chub sems//ma lus bde chen rang bzhin shwar//bar chad med pa'i ting 'dzin gyis//ma lus gnyis
47b5(sha): med 'brel par brlabs//snying po sgrib med dad pa ltar//dbang po yul sems dag po yis//byang chub sems de gzung ba yis//rang byung yis gsal bar snang //snang stong dbyer med rjes 'thun pa'i//rdzogs pa'i skye med byang chub/
47b6(sha): sems//ma lus mkha' ltar dag par snang //yis lnga rdzogs byang chub sems//dngos po lhan cig skyes pa'i sems//bde chen rang rig gnyis med pas//de bzhin nyid dri rnar snang //bsdam dang bkug dang glod pa yis//e so na mkha'
47b7(sha): lta bur 'gyur//skye 'jig gnas dmigs 'gro 'ong ga//sems lnga rdzogs par ldan par 'gyur//snying po sems dang ldan pa na//yal ga lo 'ang bzhen pa med//bde'i phyi nang gsang ba yis//phyogs bcu kun tu khyab pa dang /ga
48a1(sha): /thabs 'byung 'jug rlung gi tshogs//thabs dang ldan par nan tan bya//kun rdzob don dam gnyis med pa'i//byang chub semasu mnyam sbyar nas//don mthong 'jug pas mthar phyi man//gnyis med rdzogs pa lhun gyis
48a2(sha): grub ta sha lnga bdud rtsi ldan pa yis//long sbyang 'byung lnga byin brlabs la//bdag nyid rgyan dang long spyod pas//mnyam pa'i rgyal por the tsom med//bde chen dpa'o mdzad pa yis//bdag nyid ji ltar 'tshams pa bzhin//blang dor med par sbyang
48a3(sha): par bya/ rten pa lnga dang mi slang lnga//shes dang grub pa'i rang bzhin dag/'jigs med par dang du blang //rtse ba'i mtshan nyid smos ci dgos//a ho//zhes gsang ba'i tshig tu'o//sgyu 'phrul dra ba'i yis snying po'i rgyud las//phyag rgya chen po gas//
48a4(sha): par bstan pa'i le'u ste brgyad pa'o///de nas de bzhin gshegs pa cog du ma med pa'i bdag nyid kyis thabs dang shes rab kyi ting 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//ched du brjod pa 'di brjed do//bdag nyid byang chub sems
48a5(sha): shes shing /ma lus yos sgyu ma'i tshul//shes shing phyag rgya kun rdzogs pa//thabs dang shes rab dbyer med pas//bde chen sa gzhi dam pa dag//lha klu gnod sbyin mi la sogs//lhag pa'i skye bo dam pa la//yod 'di snying dang kyi/
48a6(sha): zhi phyir/tshig dang bden pas sgo la drang//sbyor ba'i cha las byung ba'i thabs//nges pa'i tshogs dbang du byas//tshad med tshad las bab pa dag/rig pa 'dres pa'i thig btab nas//rdzas dang tshig dang ting 'dzin gyis//sbyangs te dri med
48a7(sha): dag par bya//'byor pa'i yo byad dam pas mchod//phung po khams dang skye mched dag/ya ba yum gsal ba'i khyad par gyis//gzhi dang bcas pa lhar gsal nas//gsang dbang pad+ma byin gyis brlabs nas//dbang bskur byin
48b1(sha): brlabs dam tshig dbog/gsang ba'i dam sres gdams ngag phog/yis dkyil 'khor byin phob la//phyag rgya bzhi ldan brtul shugs bdag/rtag ldan gsal bar snang ba'i tshe//'pho 'jug thun mtshams rtag par bya//dbang po rtog/
48b2(sha): pa'i khyod par gyis//rlung gi stobs dang ldan pa yis//gzung dang zlog pas dbang bskur te//bde chen thig le khyab 'gyur na//yis snying po rang gnas pa//ji bzhin gsal bar snang bar 'gyur//snying po'i rnal mas bcings par
48b3(sha): bya//bde chen 'phel zhing rig pa nyid//zhi ba skye 'gag med pa thob//bstan na bdar mod pa yis//nges pa'i don la 'jug par bya//kun rdzob sgyu ma'i rang bzhin can//don dam ma skyes 'jig pa med//thabs dang shes rab snyoms
48b4(sha): 'jug pa//stong pa rgyas btab rig par bya//yos sgyu 'phrul rang bzhin can//thig le bden gnyis dbyer med pa'i//gsang ba'i don la thim par bya//mi g.yo brtan pa'i dbang thob nas//thabs dang shes rab dbyer med pa'o//a ho gsang
48b5(sha): ba'i tshig tu'o//sgyu 'phrul yis snying po'i rgyud las//thabs dang shes rab rdzogs pa'i dkyil 'khor gyis le'u ste dgu pa'o///de nas de bzhin gshegs pa yis rgyal po des//yis dbab pa'i ting 'dzin
48b6(sha): la snyoms par zhugs nas ched du brjod pa 'di brjod//thabs dang shes rab sbyor ba'i 'od//bskal ba'i thabs chen kun 'dus pa//sku gsung thugs kyis lam las byung //de nas ma lus khyab pa'i 'ong //bde chen snying por bstim
48b7(sha): ba las//lus kun bde bas rgyas dza gyur nas//'dar dang rdul dang baspu ldang //'phar ba sgra rtag byung na rtag/rtsa phug khyab cing stong 'gyur na//brtan pa glod pas the tsom med //bcus bcu drug co lnga dang /bco brgyad nyi shu lnga'i bar//
49a1(sha): @#/ye shes dbab pa'i sa bon can//phyin chad dngos grub yal ba yin//dngos grub gnyis med sku gsung thugs//ma lus dri med dag pa la//dngos po log pa'i kun rtag kyis//gzung dang 'dzin par zhen pa'i phyir//gang yang /
49a2(sha): de dag mkhyen mi 'gyur//de dag legs par bstan pa'i phyir//sgyu 'phrul dra ba lhun grub pa//zab mo dbang gsum khyad par gyis//rang byung gtan la 'bebs par bya//stong pa ma yin mi stong min//de bzhin dbu ma yang ma yin de//gzhi rtsa med pa'i sem/
49a3(sha): nyid/ mtshan nyid gsum ldan nyam su blang ka/snyen bsgrubs dgos 'brel bzhir ldan nas//dag pa gsum goms pa yis//'brel pa'i yis sgrub par bya//steng 'og las phye ba yis//rang rig gsang ba'i mtshan nyid dang /skyed dang rdzogs pa'i rang
49a4(sha): bzhin can//rdzogs dang rdzogs chen rang bzhin rtag/sgyu 'phrul dra ba rtog 'dod pas//bla ma'i lung dang man ngag dang //dbang dang thabs kyi khyad par dang //rgyu ba'i rlung la mkhas par bya//de dag don la mi mkhas shing //tshad ma bzhi dang mi ldan par//rang/
49a5(sha): rig gom pa med pa yis//srid pa'i sa bon mtshams sbyor nas//nyon mongs lnga'i bdag po 'gyur//phyi nang snod bcud ma lus pa//yis dkyil 'khor zhing du dgu/snyigs ma yis 'od 'bar nas//thabs dang shes rab ma nyams par gnas//srid
49a6(sha): gsum thig les khyab pa'i phyir//chos dbyings sku gsung sku gsung thugs//skal ldan rnams la bstan par bya//a ho gsang ba'i tshig tu'o//sgyu 'phrul yis snying po'i rgyud las//dbab pa dang yis bstan pa'i le'u ste bcu pa'o//
49a7(sha): de bzhin gshegs pa dgyes pa chen pos//lhun gyis grub pa'i ting 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/cheng du brjod pa 'di brjod do//thabs dang shes rob gnyis med pa'i//sgyu 'phrul dra ba bstan pa'i yid//zad ming bstan pa ye shes las//lhun grub
49b1(sha): dkyil 'khor gsal bar 'byung //phyi nang gsang ba las byung ba'i//snyen sgrub bzhi'i rim pa yis//yis sgyu ma'i rang bzhin las//'phrul pa'i dra ba cir yang snang //rang byung rig pa'i 'khor lo la//sku gsung thugs kun rdzogs par gnas/
49b2(sha): thabs dang shes rab 'brel pa'i mthus//ma chags thabs chen rdzogs par ldan//khyab pa a'i rang bzhin las//ting 'dzin 'phen pas 'tshams sbyar te//dga' dang bde ba chen po yis//nam mkha'i yis cir yang gas ye//dngas ma yas lnga'i bdag/
49b3(sha): sku gsung thugs kyi rang bzhin can//'bras bu lhun 'grub rdzogs par ldan//rgyu ba rlung gi 'phar lta gnas//snying po lam chen mtshams sbyar nas//dngas ma rnyigs mar phye byas te/dngas ma'i yes 'odu 'bar//rgyu ba las tshul du 'thun//gsang ba ni
49b4(sha): rtog pa gang snang ba//yis chen por lhun gyis grub//kun 'byung sgyu 'phrul zab mo'i dbyings//yis 'byung gnas mu mtha' med//rdzogs pa'i thig le rang bzhin dag/steng dang 'og dang gang byung ba//thabs dang shes rab gnyis med lam//khyad
49b5(sha): par a'i rang bzhin te//rin chen 'bar ba'i khong na gnas//rin chen 'phrul gyis thabs ldan pas//shal gyi sbu gur grangs sbyor ba//ring po thung ngu gang yin pa//yig de nyid mtshams sbyar bas//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams kun//yis
49b6(sha): 'byung shanas mu mtha' med//rdzogs pa'i thig ler rang bzhin dag/steng dang 'og dang gang byung ba//thabs dang shes rab gnyis med las//khyas par sna pa'i rang bzhin te//rin chen 'bar po'i khor na gnas ni/rin chen 'phrul gyi thabs ldan
49b7(sha): bas dang shes rab kyi sbu gur grangs sbyor ba//ring por thung ngu gang yin pa//yi ge de nyid mtshams sbyar bas//ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams kun//yaMs lhun gyis grub par nges//thabs dang shes rab gnyis meng pa'i//brten pa las kyi
50a1(sha): //khyang par las khro ma ma yis rdzogs par gsal//dug 'gyur sman dang mun sel bzhin//rgyud gnyis dang bzhir shes pas//yis brgyad dang cu drug dang //yang dag yas paN+DI shes bya//thabs dang shes rab gos pa yis//nyon mongs
50a2(sha): 'dod lnga kun spyad kyang ka dug chen sman du gyur pa ltar//thabs la mkhas bsang shes pa yin//lhun grub yis sbyor mkhas pas//'khor ba ma spangs zhing du dag/a ho//gsang ba'i tshig tu'o//sgyu 'phrul yis snying po'i rgyud las/lhun gyis

50a4(sha): med tshul du bzhugs pa te//thabs dang rkyen la rag las pas//de dag gnyisu snang ba yin//sku bzhi 'khor lo rang bzhin can//lus can kun la khyab par gnas//thabs kyi shes rab las sbyor ba//a li phreng ba'i bdag nyid can//ka li
50a5(sha): gnas pa'ang de bzhin te//sangs rgyas kha sbyor snying po las//'dod pa'i 'bras bu yongs kyi 'byung //chos nyid yis yon tan rtsa//rang rgyad rtsa bzhi yan lag brgyad //sa bon drug dang snying po gsum//'od la snod bcud tshul du gnas//ma rtogs

50b1(sha): /pas bstan par gas/ dus dam das bcud dkyil bajugs//dza med mkhar ltar na //gas pa la kun gyi ba da /babs bral rnams la rgyas / ldan thabs chen rnams kyi b+ha/de byung ma chen 'bang ba sa

50b3(sha): bral ba ltar//lus can kun gyis gsang bstod nas//mi mthong bde dang bral ba yin//sa bon rmu gis bdag nyid de//bshang ba'i lam la rgyas btab bo//gdams ngag 'byed sbyor ba yis//bde ba'i rang bzhin rtog par bya//bde chen snying por
